|  | (2) Imprisonment for three years to eight years shall be imposed on the offender if he obtains by the act referred to in paragraph 1 a greater benefit for himself or another. |
|---|
|  | (3) Imprisonment for four years to ten years shall be imposed on the offender if he obtains by the act referred to in paragraph 1 a considerable benefit for himself or another. |
|---|
|  | (4) Imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years shall be imposed on the offender if he obtains by the act referred to in paragraph 1 a benefit of large scale for himself or another. |
|---|
|

| § 174 |

|---|
|  | Spreading of Drug Addiction |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever induces another to abuse an addictive substance other than alcohol or supports him in it or whoever otherwise incites or spreads the abuse of such a substance shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Imprisonment for three years to eight years shall be imposed on the offender if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | b) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 175 |

|---|
|  | Serving of Alcoholic Beverages to Minors |
|---|
|  | Whoever systematically serves or serves in a larger quantity to a person younger than eighteen years alcoholic beverages shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 176 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Handling of Substances with Anabolic or Other Hormonal Effect |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever without authorisation manufactures, imports, exports, transports, offers, sells, provides to another or administers a substance with anabolic or other hormonal effect according to a special regulation for a purpose other than therapeutic treatment indicated by a physician, or whoever intermediates such activity, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, in relation to a substance with anabolic or other hormonal effect according to a special regulation, |
|---|
|  | a) without authorisation or without demonstrable health reason prescribes it on a prescription record or medical prescription or veterinary medical prescription, |
|---|
|  | b) without authorisation promotes it or without authorisation provides instructions for its use, or |
|---|
|  | c) without authorisation possesses it in a larger quantity. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for one year to five years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) although he was convicted for such an act in the preceding twelve months or was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months, |
|---|
|  | b) in connection with organised sporting activity, |
|---|
|  | c) publicly, or |
|---|
|  | d) in a significant quantity. |
|---|
|  | (4) By imprisonment for five years to twelve years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | c) on a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | d) in a large quantity. |
|---|
|  | (5) By imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm to multiple persons or death of multiple persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Failure to Provide Assistance |
|---|
|

| § 177 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever fails to provide necessary assistance to a person who is in danger of death or shows signs of serious bodily harm, although he can do so without danger to himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever fails to provide necessary assistance to a person who is in danger of death or shows signs of serious bodily harm, although he is obliged to provide such assistance by the nature of his employment, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 178 |

|---|
|  | A driver who, after a traffic accident in which he was involved, fails to provide necessary assistance to a person who suffered bodily harm in the accident, although he can do so without danger to himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | SECOND CHAPTER |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST FREEDOM AND HUMAN DIGNITY |
|---|
|  | First Part |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against Freedom |
|---|
|

| § 179 |

|---|
|  | Trafficking in Human Beings |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, using fraudulent conduct, deceit, restriction of personal freedom, abduction, violence, threat of violence, threat of other serious harm or other forms of coercion, receipt or provision of monetary payment or other benefits to obtain the consent of a person on whom another person is dependent, or abuse of his position or abuse of a defenceless or otherwise vulnerable position, recruits, transports, harbours, transfers or receives another person, even with his consent, for the purpose of his prostitution or other form of sexual exploitation including pornography, forced labour or forced service including begging, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude, forced marriage, abuse for committing criminal activity, removal of organs, tissues or cells or other forms of exploitation, shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever recruits, transports, harbours, transfers or receives a child, even with his consent, for the purpose of child prostitution or other form of sexual exploitation including child pornography, forced labour or forced service including begging, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude, forced marriage, abuse for committing criminal activity, illegal adoption, removal of organs, tissues or cells or other forms of exploitation. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for seven years to twelve years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a greater benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) and exposes another by such act to danger of serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | c) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | d) on a protected person, |
|---|
|  | e) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | f) by a more serious manner of commission, or |
|---|
|  | g) by joint action of at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (4) By imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a substantial benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes thereby serious bodily harm or death, or other particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (5) By imprisonment for twenty years to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a benefit of large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) and causes thereby serious bodily harm to multiple persons or death of multiple persons. |
|---|
|  | Entrusting a Child into the Power of Another |
|---|
|

| § 180 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever in breach of a generally binding legal regulation entrusts into the power of another a child for the purpose of adoption, or whoever in breach of a generally binding legal regulation obtains into his power a child for the purpose of adoption, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) By imprisonment for four years to ten years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a greater benefit, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years shall the offender be punished if by the act specified in paragraph 1 he causes serious bodily harm or death, or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 181 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever for remuneration entrusts a child into the power of another for the purpose of his use for child labour or for another purpose shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) By imprisonment for seven years to twelve years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a greater benefit, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm or death, or other particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | b) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (4) By imprisonment for twenty years to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment shall the offender be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby for himself or for another a benefit of large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes thereby death of multiple persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 182 |

|---|
|  | Deprivation of Personal Liberty |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully deprives another of personal liberty shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains for himself or for another a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or for another a benefit of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) and thereby causes the death of several persons. |
|---|
|

| § 183 |

|---|
|  | Restriction of Personal Liberty |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever without authorization prevents another from enjoying personal liberty shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | d) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | e) and thereby causes greater damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 184 |

|---|
|  | Restriction of Freedom of Residence |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever by deceit or violence, threat of violence or other harm |
|---|
|  | a) unlawfully compels another to reside in a certain place, or |
|---|
|  | b) unlawfully prevents another from residing in a certain place, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a public official. |
|---|
|  | (3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall be imposed on whoever unlawfully compels another to leave the territory of the Slovak Republic or unlawfully prevents another from residing in the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|

| § 185 |

|---|
|  | Hostage Taking |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever seizes a hostage and threatens to kill him or to cause him bodily harm or other harm with the aim of compelling another to do, omit or tolerate something shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) and thereby causes greater damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 186 |

|---|
|  | Extortionate Abduction |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever seizes another against his will and thereby prevents him from enjoying personal liberty, or abducts him, and by threat of his killing, bodily harm or other harm extorts from him or from a third person the provision of performance of a proprietary or non-proprietary nature, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or for another a greater benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | d) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or for another a substantial benefit, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or for another a benefit of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes the death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 187 |

|---|
|  | Abduction Abroad |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever abducts another abroad shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty-five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes the death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 188 |

|---|
|  | Robbery |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever uses violence or threat of imminent violence against another with the intent to seize another's property shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby directly endangers the life or health of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | d) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | e) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes the death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 189 |

|---|
|  | Extortion |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever compels another by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm to do, omit or tolerate something shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | d) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | e) by joint action of at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Gross Coercion |
|---|
|

| § 190 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever compels another by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm to provide performance of a proprietary or non-proprietary nature for himself or for a third person for his own services or services of a third person which he imposes upon him for such performance against his will, even if he pretends such services, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, against a group of persons on account of their nationality, race, colour, ethnic origin, age, state of health or sex or with the intention of obtaining for himself or for another unauthorised or disproportionate advantages, |
|---|
|  | a) by violence or threat of violence unlawfully compels them to do, omit or tolerate something in violation of their fundamental human rights, or |
|---|
|  | b) harasses, tortures or treats them in a similar other inhuman and cruel manner. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 191 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever compels another by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm to fulfil an obligation arising from a commitment to the performance of which a third person otherwise has a right shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 192 |

|---|
|  | Duress |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever compels another to do, omit or tolerate something by abusing his material need or urgent non-proprietary need, or distress caused by his adverse personal circumstances, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | d) with the intention of obtaining for himself or for another greater material benefit or other benefit, or |
|---|
|  | e) by denying an employee in an employment or other similar employment relationship the right to ensure health and safety at work, to holiday for recuperation or to the provision of special working conditions guaranteed by law for women and young employees. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | d) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 193 |

|---|
|  | Restriction of Freedom of Religion |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm |
|---|
|  | a) compels another to participate in a religious act, |
|---|
|  | b) without authorisation prevents another from participating in a religious act, or |
|---|
|  | c) otherwise without authorisation prevents another from exercising freedom of religion, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 194 |

|---|
|  | Violation of the Inviolability of the Home |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully enters the dwelling of another or unlawfully remains there shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | b) by overcoming an obstacle the purpose of which is to prevent entry, |
|---|
|  | c) with at least two persons, or |
|---|
|  | d) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 194a |

|---|
|  | Protection of Privacy in a Dwelling |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally violates another person's right to privacy in their dwelling, their right to private and family life conducted in their dwelling, by obtaining for themselves or other persons, through unauthorized surveillance of their dwelling, knowledge about their life and the life of persons staying in their dwelling, and by using information technology means and other technical means creates records or other documentation from this observation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to two years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) by overcoming an obstacle whose purpose is to prevent entry into the dwelling, |
|---|
|  | c) with at least two persons, or |
|---|
|  | d) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to four years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous group. |
|---|
|

| § 195 |

|---|
|  | Violation of Freedom of Association and Assembly |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever restricts another person in the exercise of their right of association or right of assembly by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, in connection with an assembly subject to notification obligation, opposes by violence or threat of imminent violence the order measures of the convener or designated organizers of such assembly, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | Violation of Secrecy of Transmitted Messages |
|---|
|

| § 196 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally violates postal secrecy by spying on or opening a sealed letter or other document transported by a postal undertaking or other customary means, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) An employee of a postal service provider who commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or intentionally enables another to commit such an act, or alters or suppresses a document transported by a postal undertaking or other customary means, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 197 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain for themselves or another an unauthorized benefit |
|---|
|  | a) discloses postal secrecy which they learned from a sealed letter or other document transported by a postal undertaking or other customary means, which were not intended for them, or |
|---|
|  | b) uses such secrecy, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) An employee of a postal service provider who commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or intentionally enables another to commit such an act, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against Human Dignity |
|---|
|

| § 199 |

|---|
|  | Rape |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever compels a woman to sexual intercourse by violence or threat of imminent violence, or whoever abuses her defencelessness for such an act, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | d) against a woman in custody or serving a sentence of imprisonment, or |
|---|
|  | e) by joint conduct of at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby causes serious bodily harm, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby directly endangers the life of a child. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 200 |

|---|
|  | Sexual Violence |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever compels another to oral contact, anal contact or other sexual practices by violence or threat of imminent violence, or whoever abuses their defencelessness for such an act, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | d) against a person in custody or serving a sentence of imprisonment, or |
|---|
|  | e) by joint conduct of at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby causes serious bodily harm, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby directly endangers the life of a child. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Sexual Abuse |
|---|
|

| § 201 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever performs sexual intercourse with a person younger than fifteen years or whoever sexually abuses such a person in another manner, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) by joint conduct of at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby causes serious bodily harm, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby directly endangers the life of a child. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 201a |

|---|
|  | Whoever proposes a personal meeting to a child younger than fifteen years through an electronic communication service with the intent to commit the criminal offence of sexual abuse or the criminal offence of production of child pornography against them, while not being a child themselves, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 201b |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever abuses a child younger than fifteen years with the intent to arouse sexual gratification by their participation in sexual activities or sexual abuse, even though such child need not directly participate in them, or whoever enables such abuse, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 by joint conduct of at least two persons. |
|---|
|

| § 202 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever induces a child to engage in extramarital intercourse or otherwise sexually abuses the child, if |
|---|
|  | a) such child is entrusted to his or her care or under his or her supervision or is a dependent person, |
|---|
|  | b) it involves child prostitution, or |
|---|
|  | c) he or she abuses a recognised position arising from trust, authority or influence over the child, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) using coercion, or |
|---|
|  | b) by joint action of at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby directly endangers the life of the child. |
|---|
|

| § 203 |

|---|
|  | Intercourse between relatives |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever engages in intercourse with a relative in the direct line or with a sibling shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a protected person. |
|---|
|  | THIRD CHAPTER |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST THE FAMILY AND YOUTH |
|---|
|

| § 204 |

|---|
|  | Bigamy |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever enters into another marriage while his or her marriage is still in force shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever knowingly enters into marriage with a person who is already in another marriage. |
|---|
|

| § 205 |

|---|
|  | Abandonment of a child |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever abandons a child for whom he or she has a duty to care and who cannot obtain help, in a place where there is no danger of threat to life or health, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a child under three years of age. |
|---|
|

| § 206 |

|---|
|  | Desertion of a child |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever deserts a child for whom he or she has a duty to care and who cannot obtain help, and thereby exposes the child to danger of death or injury to health, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a child under six years of age. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 207 |

|---|
|  | Neglect of mandatory maintenance |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever for at least two months within a period of two years fails to fulfil, even through negligence, the statutory duty to maintain or provide for another person shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever for at least two months within a period of two years intentionally evades the fulfilment of his or her statutory duty to maintain or provide for another person shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and exposes the entitled person to danger of distress, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) although he or she was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or released from imprisonment imposed for such an act. |
|---|
|

| § 208 |

|---|
|  | Abuse of a close person and entrusted person |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever causes physical suffering or psychological suffering to a close person or a person who is in his or her care or upbringing |
|---|
|  | a) by beating, kicking, striking, causing wounds and burns of various kinds, humiliation, contemptuous treatment, constant surveillance, threatening, causing fear or stress, forced isolation, emotional blackmail or other behaviour that endangers his or her physical or psychological health or restricts his or her safety, |
|---|
|  | b) by unjustified denial of food, rest or sleep or denial of necessary personal care, clothing, hygiene, health care, housing, upbringing or education, |
|---|
|  | c) by forcing to beg or to repeatedly perform an activity requiring disproportionate physical strain or psychological strain in relation to his or her age or state of health or capable of damaging his or her health, |
|---|
|  | d) by exposing to the influence of substances capable of damaging his or her health, or |
|---|
|  | e) by unjustified restriction of access to property which he or she has the right to use, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever commits a similar act to the act referred to in paragraph 1, although he or she was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) although he or she was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or released from imprisonment imposed for such an act, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious injury to health of several persons or death of several persons. |
|---|
|  | Abduction |
|---|
|

| § 209 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever removes a child or a person suffering from a mental disorder or mentally underdeveloped from the care of the person who has the duty to care for them according to law or according to an official decision shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) and thereby endangers the moral development of the abducted person. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) with the intention of taking such person abroad, |
|---|
|  | b) with the intention of obtaining for himself or herself or for another person substantial benefit, or |
|---|
|  | c) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | b) with the intention of obtaining for himself or herself or for another person benefit on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 210 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever as a parent or relative in the direct line removes a child or a person suffering from a mental disorder or mentally underdeveloped from the care of the person who has the duty to care for them according to law or according to an official decision shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|

| § 211 |

|---|
|  | Endangering the Moral Education of Youth |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever exposes, even through negligence, a person younger than eighteen years to the danger of demoralization by |
|---|
|  | a) seducing them to an idle or immoral life, |
|---|
|  | b) enabling them to lead an idle or immoral life, |
|---|
|  | c) enabling them to commit acts that are criminal offences under this Act, |
|---|
|  | d) enabling them to commit acts that are misdemeanours under special laws, or |
|---|
|  | e) preventing them from compulsory school attendance, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in subsection 1 shall be imposed on whoever, in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, employs a child younger than fifteen years, thereby preventing them from compulsory school attendance. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | TITLE FOUR |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST PROPERTY |
|---|
|

| § 212 |

|---|
|  | Theft |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever appropriates another's thing by taking possession of it and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby causes minor damage, |
|---|
|  | b) commits the act by breaking in, |
|---|
|  | c) immediately after the act attempts to retain the thing by violence or threat of imminent violence, |
|---|
|  | d) commits the act on a thing that another has on their person or with them, |
|---|
|  | e) such thing is a thing from the harvest from land that belongs to the agricultural land fund, or wood located on forest land, or fish from a pond with intensive breeding, |
|---|
|  | f) commits the act on a thing the taking of which is subject to a charge under a special regulation, or |
|---|
|  | g) was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.
|
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) although they were convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | c) at a place enjoying piety or general respect or at a place of a public assembly or ceremony, |
|---|
|  | d) on a thing that enjoys protection under a special regulation, |
|---|
|  | e) by organizing such an act, or |
|---|
|  | f) on a protected person. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 213 |

|---|
|  | Embezzlement |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever appropriates another's thing that was entrusted to them and thereby causes minor damage to another's property shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the interests of the injured party, or as a bankruptcy trustee, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a protected person. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 214 |

|---|
|  | Non-payment of Wages and Severance Pay |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a statutory body of a legal person or a natural person who is an employer, or their proxy holder, fails to pay their employee wages, salary or other remuneration for work, wage compensation or severance pay to which the employee is entitled, on the date of their maturity, although on that date they had financial resources for their payment that they did not necessarily need to ensure the activity of the legal person or the activity of the employer who is a natural person, or takes measures aimed at frustrating the payment of these financial resources, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) towards more than ten employees. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 and thereby cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 and thereby cause damage of large extent. |
|---|
|

| § 215 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorized Use of Another's Thing |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever takes possession of another's thing of minor value with the intention of temporarily using it or whoever causes minor damage to another's property by temporarily using without authorization a thing that was entrusted to them shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage or another particularly serious consequence, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they cause substantial damage by the act specified in subsection 1. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent or another particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Unauthorized Use of Another's Motor Vehicle |
|---|
|

| § 216 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever takes possession of another's motor vehicle of minor value with the intention of temporarily using it shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and was already convicted of such an act, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) in relation to a motor vehicle of greater value or by such act cause greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 in relation to a motor vehicle of substantial value or by such act cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if they commit the act specified in subsection 1 |
|---|
|  | a) in relation to a motor vehicle of value of large extent or by such act cause damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 217 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully uses another person's motor vehicle that has been entrusted to him shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and has already been convicted of such an act, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) in relation to a motor vehicle of greater value or causes greater damage by such act, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 in relation to a motor vehicle of considerable value or causes considerable damage by such act. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) in relation to a motor vehicle of large-scale value or causes large-scale damage by such act, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous group. |
|---|
|

| § 218 |

|---|
|  | Unlawful interference with the right to a house, apartment or non-residential premises |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully occupies or uses another person's house or apartment or unlawfully prevents an authorized person from using a house or apartment shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever unlawfully enters, occupies or uses another person's non-residential premises or unlawfully prevents an authorized person from using non-residential premises shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|

| § 219 |

|---|
|  | Unlawful production and use of a payment instrument |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully possesses, transports, procures or otherwise obtains or provides to another a payment instrument shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever unlawfully uses a payment instrument shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall be imposed on whoever forges, alters, imitates or unlawfully produces a payment instrument or possesses, transports, procures or otherwise obtains, uses or provides to another such a payment instrument. |
|---|
|  | (4) Whoever produces, obtains for himself or another or possesses a tool, computer program or other means specially adapted for committing the act specified in paragraph 3 shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4 on a considerable scale. |
|---|
|  | (7) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous group. |
|---|
|

| § 219a |

|---|
|  | Unlawful production, use or possession of a verification mark of a measuring instrument or security mark of a measuring instrument |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully produces, alters, imitates, combines or forges a verification mark of a measuring instrument or security mark of a measuring instrument for the purpose of using it as genuine or transports it for such purpose or provides it to another or unlawfully possesses verification marks of a measuring instrument or security marks of a measuring instrument for the purpose of using them as genuine shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever unlawfully produces, possesses, procures or otherwise obtains, provides to another or uses a stamp, tool or other means specially adapted for committing the act specified in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous group. |
|---|
|

| § 220 |

|---|
|  | Forgery and alteration of identification data of a motor vehicle |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever forges or alters the identification number of a motor vehicle, the production number of the engine of a motor vehicle, the production plate of a motor vehicle or the production number of components of a motor vehicle or uses as genuine the identification number, production number of the engine of a motor vehicle, production plate or production number of components of a motor vehicle of another motor vehicle shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and has already been convicted of such an act, |
|---|
|  | b) in relation to a motor vehicle of greater value or causes greater damage by such act, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) in relation to a motor vehicle of considerable value or causes considerable damage by such act. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) in relation to a motor vehicle of large-scale value or causes large-scale damage by such act, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous group. |
|---|
|

| § 221 |

|---|
|  | Fraud |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever enriches himself or another to the detriment of another's property by misleading someone or exploiting someone's error, and thereby causes minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes greater damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes considerable damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | d) against a protected person. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes large-scale damage, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous group, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 222 |

|---|
|  | Credit Fraud |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever obtains from another a credit or credit security by misleading them as to the fulfilment of conditions for granting the credit or for repaying the credit, and thereby causes them minor damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if, as an employee, member, statutory body, representative or other person authorised to act on behalf of the credit provider, they enable the obtaining of credit by a person whom they know does not meet the conditions specified for its provision. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 223 |

|---|
|  | Insurance Fraud |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever obtains from another an insurance benefit by misleading them as to the fulfilment of conditions for its provision, and thereby causes them minor damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if, as an employee, member, representative or other person authorised to act on behalf of the insurance benefit provider, they assist in obtaining an insurance benefit by a person whom they know does not meet the conditions specified for its provision. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 224 |

|---|
|  | Capital Fraud |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in connection with the offer, sale or distribution of securities or other instruments promising participation in the property yields of an enterprise, or whoever, in connection with an offer to increase the yields of such investment, in prospectuses or in other promotional materials or overviews concerning the property situation or yields of an enterprise in relation to a larger number of persons, states false information or unrealistic information about investment yields or about the property situation of the enterprise in which investment is to be made, or whoever conceals the disadvantages of such investment, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 225 |

|---|
|  | Subsidy Fraud |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever obtains from another a grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality, the provision or use of which is subject to conditions under a generally binding legal regulation which they do not meet, by misleading them as to their fulfilment, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever uses an obtained grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality for a purpose other than that specified. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if, as an employee, member, representative or other person authorised to act on behalf of the provider of a grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality, they enable the obtaining of a grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality by a person whom they know does not meet the conditions specified for its provision. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 and thereby cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 226 |

|---|
|  | Unlawful Enrichment |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, to the detriment of another's property, enriches themselves or another by unauthorised interference with the technical or software equipment of a computer, vending machine or other similar device or technical equipment serving for the automated execution of sale of goods, exchange or withdrawal of money or for the provision of paid performances, services, information or other benefits, achieves that goods, services or information are obtained without the required payment or money is obtained unlawfully, and thereby causes minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby cause substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 227 |

|---|
|  | Fraudulent Bankruptcy |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain an unlawful benefit for himself or another, causes the bankruptcy of a legal person in which he is a statutory body or holder of a power of attorney, by using the assets of that legal person, even if only in part, |
|---|
|  | a) to establish another legal person, or |
|---|
|  | b) to acquire its equity interest in another legal person, |
|---|
|  | or whoever acts in such a manner with the intent to cause the bankruptcy of a legal person in which he is a statutory body or holder of a power of attorney, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes greater damage or thereby obtains a greater benefit for himself or another. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage or thereby obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale or thereby obtains a benefit on a large scale for himself or another, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes another's bankruptcy. |
|---|
|

| § 228 |

|---|
|  | Culpable Bankruptcy |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever causes the bankruptcy of a legal person and frustrates the satisfaction of its creditor on a larger scale by |
|---|
|  | a) investing in a loss-making business without corresponding financial coverage, |
|---|
|  | b) accepting or providing a loan that is disadvantageous in terms of the financial possibilities of the legal person, |
|---|
|  | c) destroying, damaging, donating, concealing or otherwise removing, even if only in part, the assets of that legal person to the detriment of the creditor of the legal person, |
|---|
|  | d) using a larger part of the income from the business of the legal person for his own personal consumption, or |
|---|
|  | e) placing the income from the business of the legal person, even if only in part, into lotteries, games or bets, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever acts in the manner referred to in paragraph 1 (a) to (e), and the bankruptcy of the legal person was averted only by the provision of a contribution or other measure by a state body, territorial self-government or public law institution to which these bodies were not obliged. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes another's bankruptcy. |
|---|
|

| § 229 |

|---|
|  | Operating Dishonest Games and Bets |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever operates a monetary or other similar game or bet whose rules do not guarantee equal chances of winning to all participants shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 230 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Operation of Lotteries and Other Similar Games |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever operates a lottery or other similar game without authorisation shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Legalisation of Proceeds from Criminal Activity |
|---|
|

| § 233 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever acquires, possesses or uses a thing that is the proceeds from criminal activity committed by another person in the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever |
|---|
|  | a) conceals, transfers to himself or another a thing that is the proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad, |
|---|
|  | b) changes the nature of a thing that is the proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad, deposits it or otherwise disposes of it with the intent to enable himself or another to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or a protective measure or their execution, or |
|---|
|  | c) conceals the existence of a thing that is the proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad, in particular by concealing its origin in criminal activity, its location or ownership or other right to it. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | b) on a substantial scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) in relation to a thing originating from a particularly serious felony, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 233a |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever by negligence conceals, transfers to himself or another, possesses or uses a thing of greater value that is the proceeds from criminal activity committed by another person in the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever by negligence enables another to conceal the origin or detection of the origin of a thing of greater value that is the proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) in relation to a thing of substantial value, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in relation to a thing |
|---|
|  | a) of value on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) originating from a particularly serious felony. |
|---|
|

| § 234 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever fails to notify or report, despite such obligation arising from their employment, profession, position or function, |
|---|
|  | a) facts indicating that another person has committed the criminal offence of legalisation of proceeds of crime under § 233 or § 233a or the criminal offence of financing of terrorism under § 419c, or |
|---|
|  | b) an unusual business transaction, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The act referred to in paragraph 1 is not punishable if the offender could not make the notification or report without exposing himself or a close person to the danger of criminal prosecution. |
|---|
|

| § 235 |

|---|
|  | Usury |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, by exploiting another's distress, inexperience or mental weakness or another's agitation, causes to be provided or promised to himself or another a performance whose value is grossly disproportionate to the value of the mutual performance, or whoever asserts such a claim or transfers it to himself with the intention of asserting it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever provides a performance without authorisation, in breach of an issued authorisation or violates the legally protected rights of a consumer and causes to be provided or promised to himself or another a performance whose value is grossly disproportionate to the value of the mutual performance or whoever asserts such a claim or transfers it to himself with the intention of asserting it. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 236 |

|---|
|  | Concealment of a thing |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever appropriates another's thing of small value which has come into his possession by finding, by mistake or otherwise without the consent of the authorised person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes greater damage. |
|---|
|  | Breach of duty in the administration of another's property |
|---|
|

| § 237 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever causes minor damage to another by breaching a duty established by a generally binding legal regulation or a duty imposed by a final decision of a court or arising from a contract to care for or administer another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 238 |

|---|
|  | Whoever by negligence causes substantial damage to another by breaching a duty established by a generally binding legal regulation or a duty imposed by a final decision of a court to care for or administer another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| § 239 |

|---|
|  | Damaging a creditor |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of his creditor by |
|---|
|  | a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, selling, exchanging or otherwise removing even only part of his property, |
|---|
|  | b) encumbering a thing which is the subject of an obligation, or leasing it, |
|---|
|  | c) simulating or acknowledging a non-existent right or obligation, or assigning his claim, or assuming another's debt, even though he was not obliged or authorised to do so, or |
|---|
|  | d) simulating a reduction of his property or its extinction, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of a creditor of another person by |
|---|
|  | a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, selling, exchanging, donating or otherwise removing even only part of the debtor's property, or |
|---|
|  | b) asserting a non-existent right or claim against the debtor's property. |
|---|
