|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes another's insolvency, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|

| § 240 |

|---|
|  | Preferring a creditor |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a debtor who is not able to fulfil his due obligations, frustrates, even if only partially, the satisfaction of his creditor by preferring another creditor, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he causes damage of large extent by the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|

| § 241 |

|---|
|  | Machinations in connection with bankruptcy and composition proceedings |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a bankruptcy creditor in connection with voting on compulsory composition or as a creditor in connection with voting at a composition hearing, or as a creditor in connection with voting on the approval of a restructuring plan or a public preventive restructuring plan, accepts or causes to be promised to himself a pecuniary or other benefit, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever provides, offers or promises to a creditor in connection with voting on compulsory composition a pecuniary or other benefit for consent to the composition. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | Frustrating bankruptcy or composition proceedings |
|---|
|

| § 242 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever frustrates public preventive restructuring, bankruptcy proceedings, composition proceedings, restructuring proceedings or debt relief proceedings by |
|---|
|  | a) failing to fulfil a duty imposed on him by the law regulating such proceedings, including the duty to file a petition for declaration of bankruptcy in time, or |
|---|
|  | b) stating false information in the list of assets and liabilities, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|

| § 243 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever obstructs bankruptcy proceedings by |
|---|
|  | a) concealing property belonging to the bankruptcy estate, |
|---|
|  | b) preventing property belonging to the bankruptcy estate from being recorded and appraised, |
|---|
|  | c) failing to surrender property belonging to the bankruptcy estate, or |
|---|
|  | d) withholding, falsifying or destroying recorded information about the debtor's assets or financial activities, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes greater damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 243a |

|---|
|  | Obstruction of enforcement proceedings |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever obstructs enforcement proceedings by |
|---|
|  | a) providing false information in a declaration concerning his property, |
|---|
|  | b) preventing property subject to enforcement from being recorded and appraised, |
|---|
|  | c) withholding, falsifying, altering or destroying recorded information about the assets or financial activities of the debtor, |
|---|
|  | d) removing, damaging or destroying movable property included in the inventory, or |
|---|
|  | e) failing to surrender property subject to enforcement, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes greater damage by the act specified in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he causes damage on a large scale by the act specified in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|

| § 244 |

|---|
|  | Breach of prohibition of competition |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a person obliged to observe a prohibition of competition under law, breaches the prohibition specified in such law and thereby causes greater damage to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|  | Damaging another's property |
|---|
|

| § 245 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever destroys, damages or renders unusable another's property and thereby causes minor damage to another's assets shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) to property that enjoys protection under a special regulation, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 246 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever damages another's property by spraying, painting or inscribing it with paint or another substance shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) to property that enjoys protection under a special regulation, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 247 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised access to a computer system |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever overcomes a security measure and thereby gains unauthorised access to a computer system or part thereof shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 247a |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised interference with a computer system |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever restricts or interrupts the functioning of a computer system or part thereof |
|---|
|  | a) by unauthorised input, transmission, damage, deletion, degradation of quality, alteration, suppression or rendering inaccessible of computer data, or |
|---|
|  | b) by making unauthorised interference with the technical or software equipment of a computer and unlawfully destroying, damaging, deleting, altering or reducing the quality of information obtained, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in the activity of a state body, territorial self-government body, court or other public authority, |
|---|
|  | c) by misusing another's personal data with the aim of gaining the trust of a third party, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in critical infrastructure, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 247b |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised interference with computer data |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally damages, deletes, alters, suppresses or renders inaccessible computer data or degrades its quality within a computer system or part thereof shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in the activity of a state body, territorial self-government body, court or other public authority, |
|---|
|  | c) by misusing another's personal data with the aim of gaining the trust of a third party, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in critical infrastructure, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 247c |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Interception of Computer Data |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully intercepts computer data by technical means from non-public transmissions of computer data to, from or within a computer system, including electromagnetic emissions from a computer system containing such computer data, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, as an employee of an electronic communication service provider, commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or intentionally enables another to commit such an act, or alters or suppresses a message submitted by means of an electronic communication service, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) causes thereby considerable damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes thereby damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 247d |

|---|
|  | Production and Possession of Access Device, Password to Computer System or Other Data |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of committing the criminal offence of unauthorised access to a computer system under § 247, unauthorised interference with a computer system under § 247a, unauthorised interference with computer data under § 247b or unauthorised interception of computer data under § 247c, produces, imports, procures, purchases, sells, exchanges, puts into circulation or in any way makes available |
|---|
|  | a) a device, including a computer program, designed for unauthorised access to a computer system or part thereof, or |
|---|
|  | b) a computer password, access code or similar data enabling access to a computer system or part thereof, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) and causes thereby considerable damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes thereby damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 248 |

|---|
|  | Abuse of Ownership |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever damages an important cultural interest or other general interest by destroying, damaging, rendering unusable or squandering his own thing or part thereof which enjoys protection under a special regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and causes thereby greater damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and causes thereby considerable damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and causes thereby damage on a large scale or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 248a |

|---|
|  | Damaging and Degradation of Cultural Monument |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even through negligence, damages, degrades or destroys an immovable thing which is a cultural monument, or frustrates proceedings for the declaration of an immovable thing as a cultural monument and thereby causes considerable damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) thereby obtains for himself or for another greater benefit. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes thereby damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) in a territory enjoying protection under a special regulation. |
|---|
|

| § 249 |

|---|
|  | Damaging and Degradation of Archaeological Heritage |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully searches for, digs up, otherwise lifts from the place of discovery, moves or keeps an archaeological find, or whoever otherwise damages or degrades archaeological heritage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) with a metal detector or other detection device, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) on a larger scale, or |
|---|
|  | d) although he was sanctioned for a similar act in the preceding twelve months or was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on a considerable scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, |
|---|
|  | c) in a territory enjoying protection under a special regulation, or |
|---|
|  | d) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 249a |

|---|
|  | Forgery of Objects of Cultural Value |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully produces, imitates or alters an object of cultural value so that it would be considered genuine, or whoever procures or keeps such an object for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) by means of a computer system. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a considerable scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) if it concerns an object of cultural value enjoying protection under a special regulation. |
|---|
|  | FIFTH PART |
|---|
|  | ECONOMIC CRIMINAL OFFENCES |
|---|
|  | First Division |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Endangering Market Economy |
|---|
|

| § 250 |

|---|
|  | Abuse of Participation in Economic Competition |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever abuses participation in economic competition by |
|---|
|  | a) unfair competition in economic dealings damaging the good reputation of a competitor, or |
|---|
|  | b) conduct which is contrary to the law regulating the protection of economic competition, causing considerable damage to another competitor or endangering the operation of his undertaking, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes thereby damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes thereby the insolvency of the undertaking of another competitor, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 251 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Business Activity |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever engages in unauthorised business activity on a small scale shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) by using the labour of another person, |
|---|
|  | c) and thereby obtains greater benefit, |
|---|
|  | d) as a public official or in connection with the performance of his employment, profession or function, or |
|---|
|  | e) by providing services without professional qualifications or performing other professional activities which, according to law, may only be performed by a person who has professional competence. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 251a |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Employment |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully employs a person who is staying in the territory of the Slovak Republic in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, although he has been punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 regardless of previous punishment for a similar act |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) under particularly exploitative working conditions, including working conditions resulting from discrimination, where there is a striking disproportion in comparison with the working conditions of lawfully employed persons, which affects health and safety and is contrary to human dignity, or |
|---|
|  | d) against a person who is a victim of trafficking in human beings. |
|---|
|

| § 251b |

|---|
|  | Fraudulent Liquidation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of frustrating the resolution of termination of business by liquidation, seeks or procures another person who merely lends his name and surname and his identity to assume rights and obligations which he has no real interest in exercising, for the purpose of transferring a participation in a legal person to such person or for the purpose of appointing such person as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of a legal person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, with the intention of frustrating the resolution of termination of business by liquidation, transfers a participation in a legal person to a person who merely lends his name and surname and his identity to assume rights and obligations which he has no real interest in exercising. |
|---|
|  | (3) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever lends his name and surname and his identity for the purpose of transferring a participation in a legal person or for the purpose of appointment as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of a legal person, although he has no real interest in participation in the legal person or in acting as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of the legal person, and knew and could have known that the person who transferred the participation in the legal person to him or appointed him as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of the legal person had the intention of frustrating the resolution of termination of business of the legal person by liquidation. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraphs 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes greater damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraphs 1, 2 or 3 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraphs 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 252 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Trading in Foreign Exchange Values and Provision of Foreign Exchange Services |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully trades in foreign exchange values or unlawfully provides foreign exchange money services |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) although he has been convicted of such or a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or punished in the preceding twelve months, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains benefit on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 253 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Production of Alcohol, Tobacco and Tobacco Products |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, without authorisation, produces alcohol, tobacco or tobacco products in a larger quantity, or whoever stores or places in circulation alcohol, tobacco or tobacco products produced without authorisation in a larger quantity, shall be punished, unless it is an act subject to more severe punishment, by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever unlawfully manufactures or stores equipment for the production of alcohol, tobacco or tobacco products. |
|---|
|

| § 254 |

|---|
|  | Violation of Regulations on the Circulation of Goods in Relations with Foreign Countries |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, on a larger scale, endangers the general interest by violating a prohibition or restriction on the import, export or transit of goods shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, on a larger scale, reduces or fails to pay customs duty or other payment collected according to law upon the import of goods shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Violation of Regulations on Handling Controlled Goods and Technologies |
|---|
|

| § 255 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever violates a prohibition or restriction concerning the handling of goods and technologies controlled according to special regulations shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, without authorisation, exports goods or technologies controlled according to special regulations or transfers them to a foreign state or to an organisation having its seat abroad or to a foreign official shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years. |
|---|
|

| § 256 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever violates or fails to fulfil an important duty of their employment, profession, position or function and thereby causes the unauthorised issuance of a permit for handling goods and technologies controlled under special regulations or causes such goods to escape from records, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and for that reason the goods reached a foreign country, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | c) with the intent to obtain substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) with the intent to obtain benefit on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 257 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever obtains, on the basis of false or incomplete information, the issuance of a document necessary for authorities controlling goods and technologies under special regulations, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever destroys, damages, renders unusable, conceals or fails to keep records of documents necessary for the registration of goods and technologies controlled under special regulations, or whoever makes an intervention in the technical or software equipment of a computer in which records of such goods and technologies are kept. |
|---|
|

| § 258 |

|---|
|  | Endangering foreign exchange management |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever endangers foreign exchange management by violating foreign exchange regulations on a small scale during the period of deposit obligation or a state of emergency in foreign exchange management, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal offences against economic discipline |
|---|
|  | Distortion of economic and commercial records |
|---|
|

| § 259 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever states false or grossly distorting information or conceals mandatory information about significant facts in a statement, report, input data entered into a computer or other documents serving for |
|---|
|  | a) statistical ascertainment with the intent to secure unauthorised advantages for themselves or another, |
|---|
|  | b) employee records with the intent to secure unauthorised advantages for themselves or another, |
|---|
|  | c) accounting control, |
|---|
|  | d) control of the use of a grant, subsidy or other performance from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, from the budget of a state fund, from the budget of a higher territorial unit or from the budget of a municipality, |
|---|
|  | e) determination of the price of assets or the rate of a security upon its transfer or passage to another person, |
|---|
|  | f) resolution of imminent insolvency by public preventive restructuring or non-public preventive restructuring, bankruptcy, settlement, restructuring or debt relief, or |
|---|
|  | g) entry in the commercial register or land register, motor vehicle register or other register under a special regulation, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, with the intent referred to in paragraph 1, |
|---|
|  | a) makes an intervention in the technical or software equipment of a computer, or |
|---|
|  | b) destroys, damages, renders unusable or fails to keep the documents referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) and causes a particularly serious disruption in the functioning of the economy of the Slovak Republic or another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 260 |

|---|
|  | Whoever commits by negligence the criminal offence of distortion of economic and commercial records by conduct referred to in § 259 paragraph 1 except for letters a) or b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | Damaging the financial interests of the European Union |
|---|
|

| § 261 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever uses or presents a falsified, incorrect or incomplete statement or document, or fails to provide mandatory information, and thereby enables the unlawful retention of funds or other assets originating from the budget of the European Union, from a budget administered by the European Union or on behalf of the European Union or the use of such funds or assets for a purpose other than that intended, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever uses funds or other assets originating from the budget of the European Union, from a budget administered by the European Union or on behalf of the European Union for a purpose other than that intended. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to four years if, as an employee, member, representative or other person authorised to act on behalf of the person who provides the funds or other assets referred to in paragraph 1, they enable the acquisition of the funds or other assets referred to in paragraph 1 by a person whom they know does not meet the conditions specified for their provision, or enable their unlawful retention or use for a purpose other than that intended. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 262 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever violates or fails to fulfil a duty arising from their employment, profession, position or function in the management or control of the activities of persons managed by them, and thereby enables the commission of a criminal offence under § 261 paragraph 1, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to four years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 263 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever damages by negligence the financial interests of the European Union by conduct referred to in § 261 paragraph 1, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|

| § 264 |

|---|
|  | Endangering Commercial, Banking, Postal, Telecommunications and Tax Secrecy |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever spies out a commercial secret, banking secret, postal secret, telecommunications secret or tax secret with the intent to disclose it to an unauthorized person, or whoever intentionally discloses such a secret to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 265 |

|---|
|  | Abuse of Information in Commercial Relations |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully uses information not yet publicly accessible, which he obtained in his employment, profession, position or in his function and the disclosure of which substantially influences decision-making in commercial relations, and executes or gives an impulse to execute a contract or operation on an organized market of securities or commodities, or whoever unlawfully uses confidential information according to a special regulation or whoever discloses such confidential information to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, as an employee, member of a statutory body, partner, entrepreneur or participant in the business of two or more enterprises or legal persons with the same or similar subject of activity, with the intent specified in paragraph 1, concludes or gives an impulse to conclude a contract to the detriment of one or more of them. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 265a |

|---|
|  | Market Manipulation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully |
|---|
|  | a) gives false or grossly misleading information about the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument or a related spot contract concerning commodities, |
|---|
|  | b) causes the achievement or maintenance of the price of a financial instrument or a related spot contract concerning commodities at an unnatural or artificial level, |
|---|
|  | c) manipulates the calculation of a benchmark on the market, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, using fraudulent conduct or machinations, executes a transaction, gives an order to execute a transaction or engages in other conduct affecting the price of a financial instrument or a related spot contract concerning commodities. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 and thereby causes considerable damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Machinations in Public Procurement and Public Auction |
|---|
|

| § 266 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in connection with public procurement or public auction, with the intent to cause damage to another or to procure for himself or another a benefit, acts in breach of a generally binding legal regulation on public procurement or public auction or arranges for a competitor or participant in a public auction a preference or more advantageous conditions to the detriment of other competitors or participants in the public auction, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes considerable damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) as an announcer or organizer of a public competition or public auction, member of a privatization commission, auctioneer, |
|---|
|  | d) and demands, accepts or has promised a proprietary or other benefit, or |
|---|
|  | e) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 267 |

|---|
|  | Whoever engages in machinations in connection with public procurement by |
|---|
|  | a) compelling another by a more serious manner of commission to refrain from participation in public procurement, |
|---|
|  | b) providing, offering or promising to another a proprietary or other benefit for refraining from participation in public procurement, or |
|---|
|  | c) demanding or accepting a proprietary or other benefit for refraining from participation in public procurement, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 268 |

|---|
|  | Whoever engages in machinations in connection with a public auction of a thing by |
|---|
|  | a) compelling another by a more serious manner of commission to refrain from participation in submitting bids at the auction, |
|---|
|  | b) providing, offering or promising to another a proprietary or other benefit for refraining from submitting bids at the public auction, or |
|---|
|  | c) demanding or accepting a proprietary or other benefit for refraining from submitting bids at the public auction, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 269 |

|---|
|  | Harming a Consumer |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever harms a consumer and thereby causes him minor damage by |
|---|
|  | a) deceiving the consumer as to the quality, quantity or weight of goods or as to the type, quality and quantity of performances provided, |
|---|
|  | b) placing on the market products, works or services and thereby concealing their substantial defects, or |
|---|
|  | c) as an authorized person on the seller's side, determining a price offer on the basis of which the sale of goods or provision of services takes place at a price exceeding the limitation established by a generally binding legal regulation, or by a decision issued on its basis, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or another a greater benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) on a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission, or |
|---|
|  | d) although he was convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such an act. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains for himself or another a benefit of considerable extent or causes a disruption in supply. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains for himself or another a benefit of large extent. |
|---|
|

| § 269a |

|---|
|  | Unfair Commercial Practices Against Consumers |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever violates the rights of a consumer by conduct consisting of commercial practices which, under a special regulation on consumer protection, are considered unfair in all circumstances, |
|---|
|  | a) although he was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or released from the execution of a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such an act, or |
|---|
|  | b) although he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1, whoever violates the rights of a consumer by giving an instruction for conduct consisting of commercial practices under paragraph 1, although the seller was punished for similar conduct in the preceding twenty-four months, shall be punished. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 against a protected person. |
|---|
|  | Part Three |
|---|
|  | Offences Against Currency and Tax Offences |
|---|
|

| § 270 |

|---|
|  | Counterfeiting, Alteration and Unauthorised Production of Money and Securities |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever procures for himself or another counterfeited, altered or unauthorisedly produced money or securities, or whoever keeps such money or securities, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever counterfeits, alters or unauthorisedly produces money or securities, or whoever counterfeits, alters or unauthorisedly produces money or securities with the intention of passing them off as genuine or as money or securities of higher value, or whoever passes off counterfeited, altered or unauthorisedly produced money or securities as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 271 |

|---|
|  | Putting into Circulation Counterfeited, Altered and Unauthorisedly Produced Money and Securities |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever imports, exports, transports, receives or obtains counterfeited, altered and unauthorisedly produced money and securities with the aim of putting them into circulation shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever puts into circulation as genuine counterfeited or altered, or unauthorisedly produced money with which he was paid as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (3) In the same manner as in paragraph 2, whoever uses counterfeited or altered, or unauthorisedly produced securities as genuine shall be punished. |
|---|
|

| § 272 |

|---|
|  | Production and Possession of Counterfeiting Instruments |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever produces, procures for himself or another, or keeps an instrument or other object, or a computer program intended for counterfeiting or altering money or its security features, securities, public documents, official seals and official closures or marks, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1, whoever unauthorisedly produces, receives, obtains or keeps holograms, watermarks or other security features of money which serve to protect against their counterfeiting, using equipment or material intended for the production of money, shall be punished. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in the exercise of his profession. |
|---|
|

| § 273 |

|---|
|  | Endangering the Circulation of Money |
|---|
|  | Whoever refuses domestic money without lawful reason or damages domestic money shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months. |
|---|
|

| § 274 |

|---|
|  | Counterfeiting, Alteration and Unauthorised Production of Revenue Stamps, Postal Values, Labels and Postal Stamps |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever counterfeits, alters or unauthorisedly produces revenue stamps, domestic or foreign postal values including values withdrawn from circulation, postal labels or postal stamps with the intention of causing damage to another or procuring an unauthorised benefit for himself or another, or whoever puts such stamps, values, labels or stamps into circulation or uses them as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby a larger benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby a benefit on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 275 |

|---|
|  | Counterfeiting and Alteration of Control Technical Measures for Marking Goods |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever counterfeits or alters control stamps, control strips or other control technical measures for marking goods for tax purposes or for other purposes established by a generally binding legal regulation with the intention of causing damage to another or procuring an unauthorised benefit for himself or another, or whoever puts such control stamps into circulation or uses them as genuine, or keeps them, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby a larger benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and obtains thereby a benefit on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 276 |

|---|
|  | Reduction of Tax and Insurance Premiums |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever reduces on a small scale tax, social insurance premiums, public health insurance or contributions to old-age pension savings shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and has already been convicted of such an act, |
|---|
|  | b) and in order to facilitate the commission of such an act violates an official closure, |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | d) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a considerable scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 277 |

|---|
|  | Non-Remittance of Tax and Insurance Premiums |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever withholds on a small scale and does not remit to the designated recipient due tax, social insurance premiums, public health insurance or contributions to old-age pension savings which he deducts or collects according to law, with the intention of procuring an unauthorised benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he causes considerable damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 277a |

|---|
|  | Tax Fraud |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully claims a refund of value added tax or excise duty on a larger scale with the intent to procure an unlawful benefit for himself or another shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and has already been convicted of such an act, |
|---|
|  | b) on a substantial scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 278 |

|---|
|  | Non-payment of Tax and Insurance Premiums |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever fails to pay due tax, social insurance premiums, public health insurance premiums or contributions to old-age pension savings on a larger scale shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 278a |

|---|
|  | Frustration of Tax Administration |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever frustrates the execution of tax administration by |
|---|
|  | a) stating false or grossly distorted data in documents submitted during tax administration, or concealing mandatory data on facts decisive for the correct determination of tax, |
|---|
|  | b) altering, degrading or destroying documents decisive for the correct determination of tax, |
|---|
|  | c) failing to fulfil a notification obligation imposed on him by law, or |
|---|
|  | d) failing to fulfil an obligation imposed on him by law during a tax audit, |
|---|
|  | although he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 279 |

|---|
|  | Violation of Regulations on State Technical Measures for Marking Goods |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever handles control stamps, control strips or other control technical measures for marking goods for tax purposes or for other purposes established by law in violation of a generally binding legal regulation with the intent to cause damage to another or to procure an unlawful benefit for himself or another, or whoever, in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, imports, exports, transports, has transported, puts into circulation or stores goods without control stamps, control strips or without other control technical measures for marking them for tax purposes or for other purposes established by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 280 |

|---|
|  | Common Provision |
|---|
|  | Protection under this Part is provided, in addition to valid money, also to invalid money during the period designated for its exchange, to money not yet issued at the time of commission of the criminal offence but designated for issue into circulation, as well as to foreign money including the euro currency and foreign securities including foreign securities issued in bulk that are payable to bearer or transferable by endorsement. |
|---|
|  | Part Four |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences against Industrial Rights and against Copyright |
|---|
|

| § 281 |

|---|
|  | Infringement of Rights to Trademark, Indication of Origin of Product and Trade Name |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever puts into circulation goods or provides services unlawfully marked with a designation identical or confusingly similar to a trademark, the right to use which belongs to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, to achieve economic benefit, |
|---|
|  | a) puts into circulation goods unlawfully marked with a designation identical or confusingly similar to a registered indication of origin of product and geographical indication of product, the right to use which belongs to another, or |
|---|
|  | b) unlawfully uses a designation identical or confusingly similar to the trade name or name of a legal person or natural person. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 282 |

|---|
|  | Infringement of Industrial Rights |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully infringes rights to a patent, utility model, design, topography of a semiconductor product or to recognized plant varieties or animal breeds shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 283 |

|---|
|  | Infringement of Copyright |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully infringes legally protected rights to a work, artistic performance, sound recording or audiovisual recording, radio broadcast or television broadcast or database shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) by means of a computer system. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | TITLE SIX |
|---|
|  | GENERALLY DANGEROUS CRIMINAL OFFENCES AND OFFENCES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT |
|---|
|  | Part One |
|---|
|  | Generally Dangerous Criminal Offences |
|---|
|  | General Endangerment |
|---|
|

| § 284 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally |
|---|
|  | a) exposes people to danger of death or serious injury to health or another's property to danger of damage of large extent by causing a fire or flood, or a malfunction or accident of a means of mass transport, or a harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity, radioactivity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces, or commits another similar dangerous act (general danger), or |
|---|
|  | b) increases the general danger or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) in order to prevent or make more difficult another's exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 285 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever by negligence causes or increases a general danger, or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes |
|---|
|  | a) substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) serious injury to health or death. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons. |
|---|
|  | Damaging and endangering the operation of a facility of general benefit |
|---|
|

| § 286 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally endangers the operation of |
|---|
|  | a) a public telecommunications facility, public postal network or means of mass transport or removes or renders unusable a vertical traffic sign that is prohibitory or mandatory, |
|---|
|  | b) a protective device against the escape of pollutants, |
|---|
|  | c) an energy facility or public water supply, or public sewerage, |
|---|
|  | d) a public protective device against fire, flood or other emergency event, |
|---|
|  | e) a submarine cable or submarine pipeline, |
|---|
|  | f) a defensive or protective device against air and other similar attacks or their consequences, |
|---|
|  | g) structures and facilities serving forestry, or |
|---|
|  | h) a similar facility of general benefit, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes a disruption of the operation of a facility of general benefit, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 287 |

|---|
|  | Whoever intentionally destroys or renders unusable a clearly marked marker of a point of an astronomic-geodetic network or a basic levelling point, or a supporting gravimetric point, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|

| § 288 |

|---|
|  | Whoever by negligence endangers the operation of a facility of general benefit under § 286 with the exception of a vertical traffic sign that is prohibitory or mandatory, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months. |
|---|
|

| § 289 |

|---|
|  | Endangerment under the influence of an addictive substance |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever performs in a state excluding capability, which he brought upon himself by the influence of an addictive substance, an occupation or other activity in which he could endanger the life or health of people or cause substantial damage to property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, in the performance of an occupation or other activity referred to in paragraph 1, refuses to undergo an examination to detect an addictive substance, which is carried out by a breath test or an orientation testing device, or refuses to undergo a medical examination by taking and examining blood or other biological material, as to whether he is influenced by an addictive substance, even though this would not be associated with danger to his health during the examination. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever performs in a state excluding capability, which he brought upon himself by the influence of an addictive substance, an occupation or other activity in which he could endanger the life or health of people or cause substantial damage to property, |
|---|
|  | a) even though he was convicted of such an act or of the act referred to in paragraph 2 in the preceding twenty-four months or released from the execution of a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such an act or for the act referred to in paragraph 2, |
|---|
|  | b) even though he was punished for a similar act committed under the influence of an addictive substance or for the act referred to in paragraph 2 in the preceding twenty-four months, or |
|---|
|  | c) caused, even by negligence, injury to health to another or greater damage to another's property, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he performs in a state excluding capability, which he brought upon himself by the influence of an addictive substance, conduct in which he could endanger the life or health of people or cause substantial damage to property in the performance of an occupation or other activity in which the influence of an addictive substance is particularly dangerous, especially if he operates a means of mass transport. |
|---|
|

| § 290 |

|---|
|  | Breach of duty in imminent distress |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever prevents or makes more difficult the prevention or mitigation of imminent distress which directly affects a group of persons, by without serious reason |
|---|
|  | a) refusing assistance which is imposed on him by law or to which he has committed himself, or |
|---|
|  | b) preventing the provision of such assistance by another person, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) despite the fact that he himself caused the imminent distress. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 290a |

|---|
|  | Breach of duties during a crisis situation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during a crisis situation refuses to perform or intentionally fails to perform a duty imposed by public authorities for the defence of the state and the preservation of its security, for the protection of life and health of persons, for the protection of property, for the observance of fundamental rights and freedoms, for the prevention of threats or for the restoration of disrupted economy, in particular the proper functioning of supply, transport and public services in municipalities and the proper functioning of constitutional bodies, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 290b |

|---|
|  | Evading the Performance of Obligations During a Crisis Situation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, during a crisis situation, with the intent to evade an obligation imposed by public authorities for the defence of the state and the preservation of its security, for the protection of life and health of persons, for the protection of property, for the observance of fundamental rights and freedoms, for averting a threat or for the restoration of disrupted economy, in particular the proper functioning of supply, transport and public services in municipalities and for the proper functioning of constitutional bodies, injures himself or herself, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | Endangering the Safety of an Aircraft and a Vessel |
|---|
|

| § 291 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, on board an aircraft or a vessel, with the intent to obtain or exercise control over such means of transport |
|---|
|  | a) uses violence or the threat of imminent violence against another, |
|---|
|  | b) threatens another with killing, causing bodily harm or causing damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) abuses the defencelessness of another, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of commission. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 292 |

|---|
|  | Whoever communicates false information that may endanger the safety or operation of an aircraft in flight or a vessel during voyage shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 293 |

|---|
|  | Abduction of an Aircraft to a Foreign Country |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, for the purpose of abducting an aircraft to a foreign country, seizes such means of transport or unlawfully uses such means of transport that has been entrusted to him or her shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he or she causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | Unlawful Arming and Arms Trafficking |
|---|
|

| § 294 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, for himself or herself or another, manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, conveys, procures or possesses ammunition without authorisation, or intermediates such activity, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, for himself or herself or another, manufactures, imports, exports, conveys, procures or possesses a firearm, its part or component without authorisation or without the marking of the weapon intended for its identification according to an international treaty by which the Slovak Republic is bound, or intermediates such activity, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2 if he or she falsifies, unlawfully obscures, removes or otherwise alters the marking on a firearm intended for its identification and tracing according to an international treaty by which the Slovak Republic is bound. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3. |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, |
|---|
|  | b) on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 295 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, without authorisation |
|---|
|  | a) manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, conveys, procures or stores for himself or herself or another a weapon of mass destruction or its part or component, |
|---|
|  | b) stockpiles firearms, weapons of mass destruction, ammunition or explosives, or |
|---|
|  | c) intermediates any of the activities referred to in letter a) or b), |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 1 if he or she |
|---|
|  | a) develops, manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, conveys, procures, possesses, stores or uses an anti-personnel mine for himself or herself or another, or |
|---|
|  | b) designs a construction or operates a facility for the production of chemical weapons or biological weapons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a considerable scale. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 296 |

|---|
|  | Establishment, Organisation and Support of a Criminal Group |
|---|
|  | Whoever establishes or organises a criminal group, is a member thereof, is active for it or supports it shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years. |
|---|
|

| § 297 |

|---|
|  | Establishment, Organisation and Support of a Terrorist Group |
|---|
|  | Whoever establishes or organises a terrorist group, is a member thereof, is active for it or supports it shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years. |
|---|
|  | Unlawful Production and Possession of Nuclear Materials, Radioactive Substances, High-Risk Chemical Substances and High-Risk Biological Agents and Toxins |
|---|
|

| § 298 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even through negligence, without authorisation manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, purchases, sells, offers, exchanges, modifies, uses, gives for transport, deposits, disposes of or otherwise procures or stores for himself or herself or another nuclear or other radioactive material or a high-risk chemical substance, or a high-risk biological agent or toxin, or items intended for their production, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of commission, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains considerable benefit, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains benefit on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 299 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever manufactures, procures for himself or another, or possesses an object intended for the illicit production of nuclear or other radioactive substance, or high-risk chemical substance, or high-risk biological agent and toxin, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains greater benefit for himself or another. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains substantial benefit for himself or another. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or another. |
|---|
|

| § 299a |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised carrying out of construction |
