|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, without a building permit or in breach thereof, constructs a building or part thereof, where it does not concern a simple building or minor building under building regulations, and thereby causes serious harm to the rights or legitimate interests of the owner of land or several persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, although he was convicted of such or similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or was sanctioned in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal offences against the environment |
|---|
|  | Endangering and damaging the environment |
|---|
|

| § 300 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally endangers the environment with the risk of minor damage by violating generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever violates generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management and thereby endangers another with serious bodily harm or death, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2 if he unlawfully constructs a building in a protected area. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2 if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) in a protected area including the protection zone of natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 and thereby causes substantial damage to the environment. |
|---|
|  | (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 301 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever by negligence endangers the environment with the risk of greater damage by violating generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever by negligence violates generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management and thereby endangers another with serious bodily harm or death, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2 if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 in a protected area including the protection zone of natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 302 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised handling of waste |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even by negligence, handles waste on a small scale in breach of generally binding legal regulations, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby endangers the environment with the risk of greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby endangers another with serious bodily harm or death. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 302a |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised discharge of polluting substances |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in breach of generally binding legal regulations or an international treaty, even by negligence, discharges, or despite the fact that such obligation arises from his employment, profession, position or function, fails to prevent the discharge of oil substance, harmful liquid substance or other polluting substance from a seagoing vessel or seagoing recreational craft, |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) repeatedly, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a substantial scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) although he was already convicted of such act in the preceding twenty-four months or was sanctioned for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | Violation of the protection of waters and air |
|---|
|

| § 303 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever acts in breach of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of waters and air and causes deterioration of the quality of surface waters or groundwaters, or air in such a way that |
|---|
|  | a) he endangers another with serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) he causes the risk of substantial damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if by the act specified in paragraph 1 he causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 304 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever negligently acts in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of water or air and causes an emergency deterioration of the quality of surface water or groundwater, or air such that |
|---|
|  | a) exposes another to danger of serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) causes danger of the occurrence of substantial damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 304a |

|---|
|  | Unauthorised Production and Handling of Ozone-Depleting Substances |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even negligently, in violation of generally binding legal regulations produces, imports, exports, places into circulation or otherwise unauthorisedly uses an ozone-depleting substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 305 |

|---|
|  | Violation of the Protection of Plants and Animals |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even negligently, in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of nature and landscape or generally binding legal regulations on the protection of specimens by regulation of trade in them on a larger scale |
|---|
|  | a) damages, destroys, pulls out, digs up or collects a protected plant or damages or destroys its biotope, |
|---|
|  | b) kills, injures, catches or relocates a protected animal or damages or destroys its biotope and dwelling, |
|---|
|  | c) damages or destroys a natural biotope, or |
|---|
|  | d) endangers a protected animal species or plant species, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever in violation of generally binding regulations on the protection of nature and landscape or generally binding regulations in the field of forestry unauthorisedly drives a motor vehicle, motor tricycle, motor quad, motorcycle or scooter on forest or agricultural land, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever, even negligently, in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of nature and landscape or generally binding legal regulations on the protection of specimens by regulation of trade in them on a larger scale |
|---|
|  | a) acquires for himself or procures for another a protected animal or protected plant, or specimen, |
|---|
|  | b) keeps, cultivates, breeds, processes, imports or exports protected plants or protected animals, or specimens or trades in them, or otherwise alienates them, or |
|---|
|  | c) intentionally removes, falsifies, alters or otherwise unauthorisedly uses the unique marking of protected animals or specimens, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) on a substantial scale, |
|---|
|  | d) with the intention of obtaining for himself or another substantial benefit, or |
|---|
|  | e) although he was convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, |
|---|
|  | b) on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) with the intention of procuring for himself or another benefit on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 305a |

|---|
|  | Cruelty to Animals |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever |
|---|
|  | a) tortures an animal, although he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months or convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months, |
|---|
|  | b) tortures an animal in a particularly cruel and brutal manner, or |
|---|
|  | c) tortures an animal to death, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on several animals, |
|---|
|  | b) publicly or in a place accessible to the public, |
|---|
|  | c) on an animal specially protected by law or on an animal used for therapeutic, sporting or recreational purposes, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 305aa |

|---|
|  | Killing of a Companion Animal Without Reasonable Cause |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever without reasonable cause established by a special regulation kills a companion animal or manufactures a product of animal origin or animal by-product from a killed companion animal, trades in it or otherwise alienates it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on several animals, |
|---|
|  | b) publicly or in a place accessible to the public, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) although he was convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|

| § 305b |

|---|
|  | Neglect of Care for an Animal |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever negligently causes the death or permanent consequences to the health of an animal which he owns or for which he is obliged to care by neglecting the necessary care for this animal, although he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 regardless of whether he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, |
|---|
|  | a) on several animals, |
|---|
|  | b) publicly or in a place accessible to the public, |
|---|
|  | c) on an animal specially protected by law, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 305c |

|---|
|  | Organising Animal Fights |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever |
|---|
|  | a) organises a fight between animals, |
|---|
|  | b) for the purpose referred to in letter a) incites an animal against an animal, trains an animal on another animal, breeds, keeps, trades or otherwise handles animals or enables such conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) participates in an organised animal fight, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) publicly or in a place accessible to the public, |
|---|
|  | b) on an animal specially protected by law, |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | d) in the presence of a group of persons younger than eighteen years. |
|---|
|

| § 305d |

|---|
|  | Common Provision |
|---|
|  | (1) For the purposes of § 305a to 305c, an animal means a dog, cat, equine animal, farm animal according to a special regulation and a protected animal according to a special regulation kept in captivity. |
|---|
|  | (2) For the purposes of § 305aa, a companion animal means a dog, cat or ferret. |
|---|
|

| § 306 |

|---|
|  | Violation of the Protection of Trees and Shrubs |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in breach of generally binding legal regulations in the field of forestry or generally binding legal regulations on the protection of nature and landscape, damages or destroys a tree or shrub to a greater extent, or cuts them down, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) to a considerable extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) although he was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | b) to a large extent. |
|---|
|  | Spreading of Contagious Diseases of Animals and Plants |
|---|
|

| § 307 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even through negligence, causes a danger of introduction or spread of a contagious disease of domestic or economically important animals shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes the spread of such a disease, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes considerable damage. |
|---|
|

| § 308 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, even through negligence, causes a danger of introduction or spread of a contagious disease or pest of useful plants shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes the spread of such a disease or pest, or |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes considerable damage. |
|---|
|

| § 309 |

|---|
|  | Escape of Organisms |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in breach of generally binding legal regulations on the use of genetic technologies, causes the escape of genetically modified organisms from enclosed spaces or causes the introduction of genetically modified organisms into the environment, which may endanger people or the environment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage to the environment to a considerable extent, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or the death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 310 |

|---|
|  | Poaching |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully interferes with the exercise of the right to hunting or with the exercise of the right to fishing by hunting game or fish without permission or by hunting game or fish during their protection period or by a prohibited method, or whoever conceals, keeps, or transfers to himself or to another game or fish unlawfully hunted or found, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a mass-effective or reprehensible method, |
|---|
|  | b) to a small extent, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the environment. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and has already been convicted of such an act, |
|---|
|  | b) to a greater extent, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 to a considerable extent. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 to a large extent. |
|---|
|  | SEVENTH CHAPTER |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST THE REPUBLIC |
|---|
|  | First Part |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against the Foundations of the Republic |
|---|
|

| § 311 |

|---|
|  | High Treason |
|---|
|  | A citizen of the Slovak Republic who, in connection with a foreign power or a foreign agent, commits a criminal offence of conspiracy against the Slovak Republic, terror, subversion or sabotage, shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment. |
|---|
|

| § 312 |

|---|
|  | Conspiracy Against the Slovak Republic |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever attempts by violence or threat of violence |
|---|
|  | a) to change the constitutional order, to violate the independence or sovereignty of the Slovak Republic, or |
|---|
|  | b) to violate the territorial integrity of the Slovak Republic, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage of a large extent or other particularly serious consequence, |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping, |
|---|
|  | d) as a public official, or |
|---|
|  | e) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Terror |
|---|
|

| § 313 |

|---|
|  | Whoever, with the intent to damage the constitutional order of the Slovak Republic, intentionally kills another or attempts to do so, shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment. |
|---|
|

| § 314 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever takes a hostage and threatens to kill him or to cause him injury to health or other harm with the aim of forcing the fulfilment of conditions damaging the constitutional order of the Slovak Republic shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, |
|---|
|  | b) on a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Subversion |
|---|
|

| § 315 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intent to damage the constitutional order or defence capability of the Slovak Republic |
|---|
|  | a) exposes a group of persons to danger of death, serious injury to health or another's property to danger of damage of large extent by causing a fire, flood or malfunction, or accident of a means of mass transport, or harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity, radioactivity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces, or increases the danger, or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, or |
|---|
|  | b) commits another similar dangerous act, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of acting, or |
|---|
|  | c) against a protected person. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage of large extent or other particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 316 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intent referred to in § 315 paragraph 1, destroys, damages or renders unusable another's or his own thing, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 317 |

|---|
|  | Sabotage |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intent to damage the constitutional order or defence capability of the Slovak Republic, abuses his employment, profession, position or his function or commits another act in order to |
|---|
|  | a) frustrate or make more difficult the fulfilment of an important task of a state body, armed forces or armed corps, legal person, or |
|---|
|  | b) cause a malfunction or other serious damage in the activity of such a body or such an organisation or institution, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes greater damage, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of acting. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage of large extent or other particularly serious consequence, |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | d) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences against the Security of the Republic |
|---|
|

| § 318 |

|---|
|  | Espionage |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever spies on a fact classified for the protection of the interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of the interests of another state, international organisation, supranational organisation or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has undertaken, marked according to law with the classification level Top Secret or Secret, with the aim of disclosing it to a foreign power or foreign agent, or whoever with such aim collects data containing such a classified fact, or whoever intentionally discloses such a classified fact to a foreign power, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of an organisation whose aim is to spy on classified facts, or |
|---|
|  | b) although the safeguarding of the classified fact was specifically imposed on him. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Endangering a Classified Fact |
|---|
|

| § 319 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever spies on a fact classified for the protection of the interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of the interests of another state, international organisation, supranational organisation or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has undertaken, marked according to law with the classification level Top Secret or Secret, with the aim of disclosing it to an unauthorised person, or whoever with such aim collects data containing a classified fact, or whoever intentionally discloses such a classified fact to an unauthorised person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever spies on the classified fact referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to disclose it abroad or whoever intentionally discloses such a classified fact abroad, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) although the safeguarding of the classified fact was specifically imposed on him, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 320 |

|---|
|  | Whoever by negligence causes the disclosure of a fact classified for the protection of the interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of the interests of another state, international organisation, supranational organisation or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has undertaken, marked according to law with the classification level Top Secret or Secret, to an unauthorised person, or causes the loss of a document or thing containing such a classified fact, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | CHAPTER EIGHT |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST ORDER IN PUBLIC AFFAIRS |
|---|
|  | Part One |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences against the Exercise of Authority of a Public Authority |
|---|
|  | Attack on a Public Authority |
|---|
|

| § 321 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever uses violence with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by a more serious manner of acting. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious injury to health or death. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes damage of large extent or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 322 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever threatens another with killing, injury to health or causing minor damage |
|---|
|  | a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public authority, or |
|---|
|  | b) for the exercise of authority of a public authority, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by a more serious manner of acting. |
|---|
|  | Attack on a Public Official |
|---|
|

| § 323 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever uses violence |
|---|
|  | a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public official, or |
|---|
|  | b) for the exercise of authority of a public official, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes bodily harm thereby, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes greater damage thereby, |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | d) against a body active in criminal proceedings or a court. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes serious bodily harm or death thereby, or |
|---|
|  | b) and causes substantial damage thereby. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes the death of several persons thereby, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 324 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever threatens another with death, bodily harm or causing minor damage |
|---|
|  | a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public official, or |
|---|
|  | b) for the exercise of authority of a public official, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 325 |

|---|
|  | Protection under § 321 to 324 shall also be provided to a person who acted in support or protection of a public official. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences of Public Officials |
|---|
|

| § 326 |

|---|
|  | Abuse of Authority of a Public Official |
|---|
|  | (1) A public official who, with the intent to cause damage to another or to procure an unlawful benefit for himself or another, |
|---|
|  | a) exercises his authority in a manner contrary to law, |
|---|
|  | b) exceeds his authority, or |
|---|
|  | c) fails to fulfil an obligation arising from his authority or from a court decision, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes serious bodily harm or death thereby, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes substantial damage thereby, or |
|---|
|  | c) in order to thwart or hinder another in the exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and causes serious bodily harm to several persons or the death of several persons thereby, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes damage on a large scale thereby, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 326a |

|---|
|  | Perversion of Justice |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a judge, lay judge or arbitrator of an arbitration court, arbitrarily applies the law in deciding and thereby harms or favours another, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|

| § 327 |

|---|
|  | Frustration of a Task by a Public Official |
|---|
|  | (1) A public official who, in the exercise of his authority, negligently frustrates or substantially hinders the fulfilment of an important task, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and causes substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence thereby. |
|---|
|

| § 327a |

|---|
|  | A public official who negligently fails to fulfil an obligation arising from his authority in the administration of state property, municipal property, property of a higher territorial unit or property of a public law institution, although he knew that he could thereby violate or endanger an interest protected by this Act, but without reasonable grounds relied that he would not cause such violation or endangerment, and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby causes damage on a large scale to state property, municipal property, property of a higher territorial unit or property of a public law institution, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | Part Three |
|---|
|  | Corruption |
|---|
|  | Acceptance of a Bribe |
|---|
|

| § 328 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary accepts, demands or has promised for himself or for another person a bribe to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to violate his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 329 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in connection with the procurement of a matter of general interest, directly or through an intermediary accepts, demands or has promised for himself or for another person a bribe, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a public official. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 330 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a foreign public official, directly or through an intermediary accepts, demands or has promised for himself or for another person a bribe in connection with the performance of official duties or in connection with the exercise of his function with the intent to obtain or retain an undue advantage, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|  | Bribery |
|---|
|

| § 332 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to violate his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, or for this reason directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 333 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in connection with the procurement of a matter of general interest, directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to another, or for this reason provides, offers or promises a bribe to another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) towards a public official. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 334 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to a foreign public official or another person in connection with the performance of official duties or in connection with the performance of his function as a foreign public official with the intent to obtain or retain an undue advantage, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 336 |

|---|
|  | Indirect corruption |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary for himself or for another person accepts, requests or has promised a bribe for the fact that the bribed person will use his influence or through the influence of another act, or for the fact that he acted |
|---|
|  | a) on the exercise of authority of a public official, |
|---|
|  | b) on the exercise of authority of a foreign public official, |
|---|
|  | c) on a person in connection with the procurement of a matter of general interest, or |
|---|
|  | d) on a person to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to breach his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another for the fact that the bribed person will use his influence or through the influence of another act, or for the fact that he acted |
|---|
|  | a) on the exercise of authority of a public official, |
|---|
|  | b) on the exercise of authority of a foreign public official, |
|---|
|  | c) on a person in connection with the procurement of a matter of general interest, or |
|---|
|  | d) on a person to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to breach his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 336a |

|---|
|  | Electoral corruption |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to a person who has the right to vote, participate in a referendum or in a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic, in order that he |
|---|
|  | a) vote or cast a vote in a certain manner, |
|---|
|  | b) not vote or not cast a vote in a certain manner, |
|---|
|  | c) not vote or not cast a vote at all, or |
|---|
|  | d) not participate in elections, a referendum or a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic, or for this reason directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to another person, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever in connection with the exercise of the right to vote, participate in a referendum or in a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic directly or through an intermediary for himself or another person accepts, requests or has promised a bribe, in order that he |
|---|
|  | a) vote or cast a vote in a certain manner, |
|---|
|  | b) not vote or not cast a vote in a certain manner, |
|---|
|  | c) not vote or not cast a vote at all, or |
|---|
|  | d) not participate in elections, a referendum or a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.
|
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | c) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | d) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 336b |

|---|
|  | Sports corruption |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to influence the course of a competition or the result of a competition, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever directly or through an intermediary for himself or for another person accepts, requests or has promised a bribe to act or refrain from acting and thereby influence the course or result of a competition. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) although he was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) on a larger scale. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) as a referee, delegate of a sports federation or official of a sports organization, |
|---|
|  | b) in a competition organized by an international sports organization, or |
|---|
|  | c) on a substantial scale. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 on a large scale. |
|---|
|  | Acceptance and provision of undue advantage |
|---|
|

| § 336c |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever as a public official directly or through an intermediary for himself or for another person accepts, requests or has promised an undue advantage in connection with his position or function, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of greater value. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of the value of a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 336d |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises an undue advantage to a public official or another person in connection with the position or function of a public official, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of greater value. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of the value of a large scale. |
|---|
|  | Part Four |
|---|
|  | Certain forms of criminal complicity |
|---|
|

| § 337 |

|---|
|  | Incitement |
|---|
|  | Whoever publicly incites to a criminal offence or publicly calls for mass non-performance of an important obligation imposed by law or on the basis thereof, or for serious breach of public order, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| § 338 |

|---|
|  | Approval of a criminal offence |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever publicly approves a criminal offence or publicly praises its perpetrator for a criminal offence, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever with the intent to express agreement with a criminal offence |
|---|
|  | a) rewards or compensates the perpetrator or a person close to him for the punishment, or |
|---|
|  | b) collects funds for such reward or compensation. |
|---|
|

| § 339 |

|---|
|  | Harbouring |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever helps a perpetrator of a criminal offence with the intent to enable him to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or protective measure or their execution, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years; however, if he thus helps a perpetrator of a criminal offence for which this Act establishes a milder punishment, he shall be punished by this milder punishment. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in favour of a person close to him shall not be punishable, except in the case that he would do so with the intent to |
|---|
|  | a) help a person who committed a criminal offence of high treason under § 311, subversion against the Slovak Republic under § 312, terror under § 313 and § 314, sedition under § 315 and § 316, sabotage under § 317, espionage under § 318 or genocide under § 418, or |
|---|
|  | b) procure for himself or another a pecuniary advantage. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable if he was forced to provide help and could not refuse it without exposing himself or a person close to him to danger of death, injury to health or other serious harm. |
|---|
|

| § 340 |

|---|
|  | Failure to report a criminal offence |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever learns in a credible manner that another has committed a felony for which this Act establishes a punishment of imprisonment with an upper limit of at least ten years, or any of the corruption offences referred to in Part Three of Chapter Eight of the Special Part, and does not report such felony or criminal offence without delay to an authority active in criminal proceedings or to the Police Force, or instead, if it concerns a soldier, to his superior or service authority, and if it concerns a person in execution of a sentence of imprisonment or in execution of custody to a member of the Prison and Court Guard Service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable if he could not make the report without exposing himself or a person close to him to danger of death, injury to health, other serious harm or criminal prosecution. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable if by reporting the criminal offence he would violate |
|---|
|  | a) the seal of confession or the secrecy of information entrusted to him orally or in writing under condition of confidentiality as a person entrusted with pastoral activity, or |
|---|
|  | b) a duty of confidentiality recognised by law. |
|---|
|

| § 341 |

|---|
|  | Failure to prevent a criminal offence |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever learns in a credible manner that another is preparing or committing a felony for which this Act establishes a punishment of imprisonment with an upper limit of the sentence of at least ten years, or any of the corruption offences referred to in Part Three of Chapter Eight of the Special Part, and does not prevent the commission or completion of such felony or criminal offence personally or through another capable person or the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be punishable if he could not prevent the felony without considerable difficulty or without exposing himself or a person close to him to danger of death, serious injury to health, other serious harm or criminal prosecution. However, exposing a person close to him to danger of criminal prosecution does not relieve the perpetrator of criminal liability if it concerns failure to prevent a felony for which this Act allows imposition of a sentence of life imprisonment. |
|---|
|  | (3) A felony may also be prevented by its timely report to an authority active in criminal proceedings or to the Police Force; a soldier may instead make the report to a superior or service authority and a person in execution of a sentence of imprisonment or in execution of custody to a member of the Prison and Court Guard Service. |
|---|
|  | (4) The obligation under paragraph 1 does not apply to a person if by fulfilling it he would violate the seal of confession. |
|---|
|  | Part Five |
|---|
|  | Other forms of disruption of the activity of public authorities |
|---|
|

| § 342 |

|---|
|  | Interference with the independence of the court |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever influences a judge not to fulfil his duty in proceedings before a court, or whoever acts with the intent to defeat the rights of participants in court proceedings or of the accused to a lawful judge, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) in order to defeat or hinder another in the exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms, |
|---|
|  | c) as a public official or other person in connection with the performance of his employment, profession, position or function, or |
|---|
|  | d) as a person exercising the authority of a body for the management and administration of courts. |
|---|
|  | (3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to procure for himself or another a substantial advantage or to cause substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 343 |

|---|
|  | Contempt of court |
|---|
|  | Whoever by repeated conduct despite prior warning |
|---|
|  | a) seriously disrupts court proceedings, |
|---|
|  | b) behaves insultingly towards the court at the hearing or disparages the court, or |
|---|
|  | c) without sufficient excuse disobeys an order of the court or its summons, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| § 344 |

|---|
|  | Obstruction of justice |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever in proceedings before a court or in criminal proceedings |
|---|
|  | a) presents evidence which he knows to be falsified or altered, for the purpose of using it as genuine, |
|---|
|  | b) falsifies, alters or defeats evidence, or prevents the obtaining of evidence, |
|---|
|  | c) defeats or prevents the presence or testimony of a party to criminal proceedings, a participant in court proceedings, or their representatives in the proceedings, a witness, expert, interpreter or translator, or |
|---|
|  | d) uses violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm in order to influence a judge, a party to criminal proceedings, a participant in court proceedings, a witness, expert, interpreter, translator or an authority active in criminal proceedings, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) with the intent to procure for himself or another a substantial advantage or to cause substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence, |
|---|
|  | b) with the intent to defeat or hinder another in the exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | d) as a public official or other person in connection with the performance of his employment, profession, position or function, or |
|---|
|  | e) as a person exercising the authority of a body for the management and administration of courts. |
|---|
|

| § 345 |

|---|
|  | False accusation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever falsely accuses another of a criminal offence with the intent to bring about his criminal prosecution shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | b) publicly. |
|---|
|  | (3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 346 |

|---|
|  | False Testimony and False Oath |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as a witness in proceedings before a court or in criminal proceedings or for the purposes of criminal proceedings abroad before a prosecutor or police officer, or before a judge of an international body recognised by the Slovak Republic, states an untruth about a circumstance that is of substantial significance for a decision, or whoever conceals such a circumstance, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, in criminal proceedings before a court or for the purposes of criminal proceedings abroad, after taking an oath, states an untruth about a circumstance that is of substantial significance for a decision, or conceals such a circumstance, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 347 |

|---|
|  | False Expert Opinion, Interpreting Act and Translation Act |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, as an expert, interpreter or translator |
|---|
|  | a) before a court, prosecutor or police officer in criminal proceedings or before a court in civil proceedings and in administrative judicial proceedings, or in enforcement proceedings, or in proceedings before a public administration body, or before an arbitration court, states an untruth about a circumstance that is of substantial significance for a decision, or conceals such a circumstance, or |
|---|
|  | b) when submitting an expert opinion or performing an interpreting or translation act on the basis of a contract, causes minor damage to another by stating an untruth about a circumstance that is of substantial significance for the person to whom the expert opinion or interpreting or translation act relates, or is of substantial significance for a decision for which the expert opinion or interpreting or translation act is a basis, or if he conceals such a circumstance, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|  | Frustration of Enforcement of Official Decision |
|---|
|

| § 348 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever frustrates or substantially impedes the enforcement of a decision of a court or other public authority by |
|---|
|  | a) failing to commence the execution of a sentence of imprisonment within the time limit set by the court without serious reason, |
|---|
|  | b) staying without permission and without serious reason in a place or district to which this sentence applies during the execution of a sentence of prohibition of residence, or failing to comply with the restrictions and obligations imposed on him by the court in connection with the execution of this sentence, |
|---|
|  | c) staying without permission and without serious reason unlawfully on the territory of the Slovak Republic, although he has been sentenced to expulsion or his stay on the territory of the Slovak Republic has been prohibited, |
|---|
|  | d) carrying out an activity to which a decision of a court or other state body on prohibition of activity applies, |
|---|
|  | e) applying for or receiving a grant, subsidy or aid and support provided from European Union funds despite a final decision of a court prohibiting the receipt of grants, subsidies or aid and support provided from European Union funds, or participating in public procurement despite a final decision of a court prohibiting participation in public procurement, |
|---|
|  | f) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of protective treatment or protective education imposed on him by a court, or otherwise, in particular by escaping from an institution, substantially impeding the enforcement of such decisions, |
|---|
|  | g) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of detention or sentence, |
|---|
|  | h) engaging in serious or repeated conduct to frustrate an eviction from a common dwelling issued pursuant to a special regulation or on the basis of an urgent measure of a court, |
|---|
|  | i) engaging in serious or repeated conduct to frustrate a prohibition or restriction of contact, entry or approach issued and certified pursuant to a special regulation or issued on the basis of a court decision in civil proceedings, |
|---|
|  | j) participating in a public event to which a sentence of prohibition of participation in public events applies or failing to comply with the restrictions and obligations imposed on him by the court in connection with the execution of this sentence, |
|---|
|  | k) engaging in serious conduct by which he frustrates the execution of imposed protective supervision, or |
|---|
|  | l) carrying out works, modifications or otherwise continuing the realisation of a building or its alteration despite a final decision prohibiting the continuation of works, modifications or the realisation of a building or its alteration, where it is not a simple building or minor building under building regulations, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever frustrates or substantially impedes in criminal proceedings the enforcement of a decision of a court or other public authority by |
|---|
|  | a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, misappropriating or removing a thing to which such a decision relates, or |
|---|
|  | b) escaping from guard, from detention or from the execution of a sentence of imprisonment or assisting another in such an escape, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 349 |

|---|
|  | Whoever, after measures in civil proceedings aimed at enforcing a court decision or a court-approved agreement on the upbringing of minor children have been used against him without success, frustrates the enforcement of such a decision or agreement, or whoever frustrates the enforcement of an urgent measure imposed in civil proceedings for the protection of persons threatened by violence or in matters of court care for minors, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 350 |

|---|
|  | Frustration of Enforcement of Decision of the Constitutional Court of the Slovak Republic |
|---|
|  | Whoever frustrates or substantially impedes the enforcement of a decision of the Constitutional Court of the Slovak Republic by failing to fulfil an obligation arising from its decision shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| § 351 |

|---|
|  | Frustration of Preparation and Conduct of Elections and Referendum |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever prevents another by violence or threat of violence, or by deceit, from exercising the constitutional right to vote or to vote in a referendum, or forces another in such a manner to exercise such constitutional rights, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, in connection with the exercise of the constitutional rights referred to in paragraph 1, |
|---|
|  | a) knowingly counts votes incorrectly or violates the secrecy of voting, or |
|---|
|  | b) otherwise frustrates the exercise of these constitutional rights in a gross manner. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | d) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 352 |

|---|
|  | Forgery and Alteration of Public Documents, Official Seals, Official Closures, Official Emblems and Official Marks |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever forges a public document, official seal, official closure, official emblem, official mark or stamp, substantially alters their content with the intent that they be used as genuine, or uses them as genuine, or has them produced with the intent to use them as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever obtains the issuance of a genuine public document, official seal, official closure, official emblem, official mark or stamp on the basis of false documents. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in a more serious manner. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes damage on a large scale or other particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|  | (6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (7) Protection under paragraphs 1 to 6 shall also be afforded to foreign public documents, official seals, official closures, official emblems or official marks. |
|---|
|

| § 352a |

|---|
|  | Forgery and Production of False Medical Documentation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever forges medical documentation or intentionally produces false medical documentation with the intent to use it as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, or uses it as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, or whoever has such medical documentation produced with the intent to use it as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, or uses such medical documentation as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or another a greater benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes greater damage, |
|---|
|  | c) as a healthcare professional, |
|---|
|  | d) in a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | e) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby obtains for himself or another a substantial benefit, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and |
|---|
|  | a) thereby obtains for himself or another a large benefit, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 353 |

|---|
|  | Endangering Confidential Facts and Restricted Facts |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever spies on a fact classified for the protection of the interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of the interests of another state, international organization, supranational organization or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has undertaken, marked according to law with the classification level Confidential or Restricted, with the aim of disclosing it to an unauthorized person, or whoever with such aim collects data containing such classified fact, or whoever intentionally discloses such classified fact to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever spies on the classified fact referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to disclose it abroad or whoever intentionally discloses such classified fact abroad shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 354 |

|---|
|  | Violent Crossing of State Border |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever crosses the state border using violence or threat of imminent violence shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) in a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) with at least two persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) by organizing such act. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a soldier during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | People Smuggling |
|---|
|

| § 355 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever for a person who is not a citizen of the Slovak Republic or a person with permanent residence on the territory of the Slovak Republic organizes the unlawful crossing of the state border of the Slovak Republic or passage through its territory, or enables such conduct, or assists in it, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever with the intent to obtain for himself or another directly or indirectly a financial advantage or other material advantage for a person who is not a citizen of the Slovak Republic or a person with permanent residence on the territory of the Slovak Republic, |
|---|
|  | a) organizes the unlawful crossing of the state border of the Slovak Republic or passage through its territory, or enables such conduct, or assists in it, or |
|---|
|  | b) for the purpose under letter a) produces, procures, provides or holds a false travel document or false identity document, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains for himself or another a greater benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) in a manner that may endanger the lives and health of smuggled persons or means inhuman treatment or degrading treatment, or abuse of smuggled persons, or |
|---|
|  | d) in a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains for himself or another a substantial benefit, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains a benefit on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 356 |

|---|
|  | Whoever with the intent to obtain for himself or another directly or indirectly a financial advantage or other material advantage enables or assists a person who is not a citizen of the Slovak Republic or a citizen of another Member State of the European Union or a citizen of a contracting state of the Agreement on the European Economic Area, or does not have permanent residence on their territory, to remain or to be unlawfully employed on the territory of the Slovak Republic, another Member State of the European Union or a contracting state of the Agreement on the European Economic Area, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. |
|---|
|

| § 357 |

|---|
|  | Unlawful Crossing of State Border |
|---|
|  | Whoever violates regulations on international flights by entering the territory of the Slovak Republic by air transport, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 358 |

|---|
|  | Mutiny of Prisoners |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever participates in resistance by a group of prisoners against a supervisory authority, its order or prison regulations, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he organises the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | CHAPTER NINE |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST OTHER RIGHTS AND FREEDOMS |
|---|
|

| § 359 |

|---|
|  | Violence Against a Group of Inhabitants |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever threatens a group of inhabitants with death, serious bodily harm or other serious harm, or with causing damage on a large scale, or whoever uses violence against a group of inhabitants, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 360 |

|---|
|  | Dangerous Threatening |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever threatens another with death, serious bodily harm or other serious harm in such a manner that it may arouse justified apprehension, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) in order to thwart or make difficult another's exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms, |
|---|
|  | d) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | e) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 360a |

|---|
|  | Dangerous Stalking |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever stalks another for a long time in such a manner that it may arouse justified apprehension for his life or health, the life or health of a person close to him or substantially worsen the quality of his life, by |
|---|
|  | a) threatening to harm the health or cause other harm to him or a person close to him, |
|---|
|  | b) seeking his personal proximity or following him, |
|---|
|  | c) contacting him through a third person or by electronic communication service, in writing or otherwise against his will, |
|---|
|  | d) misusing his personal data for the purpose of obtaining personal or other contact, or |
|---|
|  | e) otherwise restricting him in his usual way of life, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, or
|
|---|
|  | d) publicly. |
|---|
|

| § 360b |

|---|
|  | Dangerous Electronic Harassment |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally substantially worsens the quality of life of another by means of an electronic communication service, computer system or computer network by |
|---|
|  | a) humiliating, intimidating, acting without authorisation in his name or otherwise harassing him for a long time, or |
|---|
|  | b) without authorisation publishing or making available to a third person a visual, audio or audio-visual recording of his expression of a personal nature obtained with his consent, capable of substantially endangering his dignity or causing him other serious harm to his rights, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to four years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) with the intention of obtaining substantial benefit for himself or another, or |
|---|
|  | c) although he was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months. |
|---|
|  | Spreading an Alarming Message |
|---|
|

| § 361 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever intentionally causes a danger of serious disturbance to at least part of the population of a place by spreading an alarming message which is false, or commits another similar act capable of causing such danger, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever reports a message or other similar act referred to in paragraph 1, although he knows that it is false and may trigger a measure leading to a danger of serious disturbance to at least part of the population of a place, to a legal person or to the Police Force or another state authority or to a mass media, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and has already been convicted of such an act, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in the economic operation or economic activity of a legal person or in the activity of a state authority or another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 362 |

|---|
|  | Whoever during a crisis situation of the state causes, even through negligence, a danger of serious disturbance, despondency or defeatist mood in at least part of the population of a place by spreading an alarming message, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|

| § 363 |

|---|
|  | Intoxication |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever by consumption or application of an addictive substance, even through negligence, brings himself into a state of insanity in which he commits conduct which otherwise has the characteristics of a criminal offence, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years; however, if he commits conduct which otherwise has the characteristics of a criminal offence for which the law prescribes a milder punishment, he shall be punished by this milder punishment. |
|---|
|  | (2) The provision of paragraph 1, as well as § 23, shall not apply if the offender brought himself into a state of insanity with the intention of committing a criminal offence. |
|---|
|

| § 364 |

|---|
|  | Hooliganism |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever commits verbally or physically, publicly or in a place accessible to the public, gross indecency or hooliganism, in particular by |
|---|
|  | a) assaulting another, |
|---|
