|  | b) defaming a state symbol, |
|---|
|  | c) defaming a historical or cultural monument, |
|---|
|  | d) grossly disrupting the course of a public event, in particular by unauthorised use of a pyrotechnic product, violent damage to a seat or part of a sports facility serving to separate sectors, throwing a dangerous object onto a sports field or other place where an event is held, or |
|---|
|  | e) causing public scandal by performing sexual intercourse or performing sexual exhibitionism or other pathological sexual practices, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) in the presence of a group of persons younger than eighteen years, |
|---|
|  | d) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | e) although he was convicted of such or a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or punished in the preceding twelve months. |
|---|
|

| § 365 |

|---|
|  | Desecration of a Place of Final Rest |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever destroys, damages or desecrates a grave, an urn with human remains, a monument or a tombstone or destroys or damages the facilities of a burial ground or other place of final rest, or whoever commits other gross indecency or hooliganism at a burial ground or other place of final rest, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage. |
|---|
|

| § 366 |

|---|
|  | Desecration of the Dead |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever abuses or desecrates a dead person, or unlawfully performs an exhumation of human remains, or arbitrarily removes human remains from a burial site, or handles human remains in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive. |
|---|
|

| § 367 |

|---|
|  | Procuring |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever procures, induces, seduces, exploits, recruits or offers another for the practice of prostitution, or whoever profits from prostitution practiced by another, or enables its practice, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits such an act by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 against a protected person. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death. |
|---|
|

| § 368 |

|---|
|  | Production of Child Pornography |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever exploits, recruits, offers or otherwise abuses a child for the production of child pornography or a child pornographic performance or enables such abuse, or otherwise participates in such production, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) against a child younger than twelve years, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) publicly, |
|---|
|  | d) by joint conduct of at least two persons, or |
|---|
|  | e) and thereby directly endangers the life of the child. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to multiple persons or death of multiple persons, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby obtains a benefit of large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 369 |

|---|
|  | Distribution of Child Pornography |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever reproduces, transports, procures, makes accessible or otherwise distributes child pornography shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) publicly. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains a benefit of large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 370 |

|---|
|  | Possession of Child Pornography and Participation in a Child Pornographic Performance |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever possesses child pornography or whoever acts with the intent to gain access to child pornography through an electronic communication service shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever intentionally participates in a child pornographic performance. |
|---|
|  | Endangering Morality |
|---|
|

| § 371 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever produces, purchases, imports or otherwise procures and subsequently sells, lends or otherwise puts into circulation, distributes, makes publicly accessible or publishes pornography, sound or image carriers, depictions or other objects endangering morality, in which disrespect for human beings and violence is manifested or which depict sexual intercourse with an animal or other sexual pathological practices, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) publicly. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|

| § 372 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever pornography |
|---|
|  | a) offers, surrenders or sells to a child, or |
|---|
|  | b) displays or otherwise makes accessible in a place accessible to children, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) publicly. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains a substantial benefit for himself or another, or |
|---|
|  | b) by offering, making accessible or displaying pornography, sound or image carriers or depictions in which disrespect for human beings and violence is manifested or which depict sexual intercourse with an animal or other sexual pathological practices. |
|---|
|

| § 372a |

|---|
|  | Support and Promotion of Sexual Pathological Practices |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever supports or whoever publicly or in a place accessible to the public promotes, approves, grossly trivializes or attempts to justify sexual intercourse |
|---|
|  | a) with a child, |
|---|
|  | b) with a relative in direct line or with a sibling, |
|---|
|  | c) with a dead person, |
|---|
|  | d) with an animal, or |
|---|
|  | e) other sexual pathological practices, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever supports or whoever publicly or in a place accessible to the public promotes, approves, grossly trivializes or attempts to justify pedophilia, necrophilia or zoophilia. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever commits the act specified in paragraph 2 is not punishable if the promotion was demonstrably carried out for the purpose of educational, therapeutic or research activities. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby obtains a substantial benefit, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes serious harm to the rights of the affected person, |
|---|
|  | c) although he was convicted for such or a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or punished in the preceding twelve months, or |
|---|
|  | d) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|

| § 373 |

|---|
|  | Defamation |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever communicates about another an untrue fact that is capable of substantially endangering his reputation among fellow citizens, harming him in employment, in business, disrupting his family relations or causing him other serious harm, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | c) publicly, or |
|---|
|  | d) in business by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes damage of large scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes another loss of employment, bankruptcy of a business or divorce of a marriage. |
|---|
|

| § 374 |

|---|
|  | Unauthorized Handling of Personal Data |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever unlawfully provides, makes accessible or discloses |
|---|
|  | a) personal data about another person collected in connection with the exercise of public authority or the assertion of constitutional rights of a person, or |
|---|
|  | b) personal data about another person obtained in connection with the exercise of their profession, employment or function |
|---|
|  | and thereby violates a duty established by a generally binding legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious harm to the rights of the affected person, |
|---|
|  | b) publicly, or |
|---|
|  | c) by a more serious manner of conduct. |
|---|
|  | Damaging the Rights of Others |
|---|
|

| § 375 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever causes serious harm to the rights of another by |
|---|
|  | a) misleading someone or |
|---|
|  | b) exploiting someone's error, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | c) by passing himself off as a public official. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains for himself or for another a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|

| § 376 |

|---|
|  | Whoever unlawfully violates the secrecy of a letter or other written document, sound recording, image recording or other recording, computer data or other document kept in the privacy of another by disclosing it or making it accessible to a third person or otherwise using it and thereby causes serious harm to the rights of another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| § 377 |

|---|
|  | Violation of Confidentiality of Oral Expression and Other Expression of a Personal Nature |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever violates the confidentiality of words spoken in private or other expression of a personal nature by unlawfully capturing it with a recording device and making the recording thus made accessible to a third person or otherwise using it and thereby causes serious harm to the rights of another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of an organized group, |
|---|
|  | b) and causes substantial damage by such act, or |
|---|
|  | c) with the intent to obtain for himself or another a substantial benefit. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if |
|---|
|  | a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a public official, |
|---|
|  | b) he causes damage on a large scale by such act, or |
|---|
|  | c) he commits such act with the intent to obtain for himself or for another a benefit on a large scale. |
|---|
|  | TENTH CHAPTER |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST CONSCRIPTION, AGAINST CIVILIAN SERVICE, AGAINST SERVICE IN THE ARMED FORCES AND AGAINST THE DEFENCE OF THE HOMELAND |
|---|
|  | Part One |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against Conscription |
|---|
|

| § 379 |

|---|
|  | Frustrating Fitness for Service |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever renders himself or another permanently or temporarily unfit or less fit for the performance of conscription duty or other duties for the defence of the homeland, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 380 |

|---|
|  | Failure to Fulfil Conscription Duty |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever fails to report for conscription or does not undergo medical examination with the intent to fail to fulfil his conscription duty, or to delay its fulfilment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 381 |

|---|
|  | Evading Conscription Duty |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever engages in machinations with the intent to obtain relief in the fulfilment of conscription duty, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever engages in machinations with the intent to |
|---|
|  | a) himself evade wholly or partly the fulfilment of conscription duty, or |
|---|
|  | b) enable another to evade wholly or partly the fulfilment of conscription duty, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against Civilian Service |
|---|
|  | Failure to Commence Civilian Service |
|---|
|

| § 382 |

|---|
|  | Whoever with the intent to evade civilian service fails to commence it within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in the call-up order, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 383 |

|---|
|  | Whoever fails to commence, even through negligence, civilian service within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in the call-up order, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | Evading the Performance of Civilian Service |
|---|
|

| § 384 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever with the intent to evade the performance of civilian service or the fulfilment of duties arising therefrom injures himself, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another subterfuge, or whoever refuses to perform civilian service, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever refuses to comply with instructions in the performance of civilian service or systematically fails to comply with such instructions, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| § 385 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever without permission leaves the place of performance of civilian service for a period longer than 48 hours, or stays without permission outside this place for such period, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever without permission leaves the place of performance of civilian service for a period longer than 14 days, or stays without permission outside this place for such period, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever with the intent to evade the performance of civilian service leaves the place of performance of this service or with such intent stays outside this place, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. |
|---|
|  | Part Three |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against Service in the Armed Forces |
|---|
|  | Failure to Commence Service in the Armed Forces |
|---|
|

| § 386 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever with the intent to evade military service fails to commence service in the armed forces within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in the call-up order, shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 387 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever fails to commence, even through negligence, service in the armed forces within 24 hours after the expiry of the time limit specified in the call-up order, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 388 |

|---|
|  | Whoever after the declaration of mobilization of the armed forces, even through negligence, fails to report without delay from abroad to commence military service, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|

| § 389 |

|---|
|  | Violation of Personal and Material Obligations |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, after the declaration of mobilization of the armed forces, fails to fulfill, even through negligence, a personal or material obligation, or an obligation to provide accommodation for the defense of the homeland, or intentionally evades the fulfillment of such an obligation, or frustrates or impedes the fulfillment of such an obligation by another person, even through negligence, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if, by the act specified in paragraph 1, he endangers to a considerable extent the interests of the defense of the homeland. |
|---|
|  | Part Four |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offenses Against the Defense of the Homeland |
|---|
|

| § 390 |

|---|
|  | Collaboration with the Enemy |
|---|
|  | Whoever, during a state of war or war, procures an advantage for the enemy or supports him in any way, shall be punished, if the act does not constitute a more severely punishable offense, by imprisonment for one year to ten years. |
|---|
|

| § 391 |

|---|
|  | War Treason |
|---|
|  | A citizen of the Slovak Republic who, during a state of war or war, serves in an enemy army or in an enemy armed corps, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment. |
|---|
|

| § 392 |

|---|
|  | Service in a Foreign Army |
|---|
|  | (1) A citizen of the Slovak Republic who serves in a foreign army without permission shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a state of war or war. |
|---|
|  | CHAPTER ELEVEN |
|---|
|  | MILITARY CRIMINAL OFFENSES |
|---|
|  | Part One |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offenses Against Military Subordination and Military Honor |
|---|
|  | Disobedience of an Order |
|---|
|

| § 393 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever refuses to execute or intentionally fails to execute an order shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) with a group of soldiers, |
|---|
|  | b) with a weapon, |
|---|
|  | c) in circumstances where he could thereby frustrate or substantially impede the fulfillment of an important service task, or |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2 if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes endangerment of the combat readiness of a unit or another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 394 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, through negligence, fails to execute an order in circumstances where he could thereby frustrate or substantially impede the fulfillment of an important service task shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | b) and thereby causes endangerment of the combat readiness of a unit or another particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 395 |

|---|
|  | Resistance or Coercion to Violate a Military Duty |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever resists a soldier who is fulfilling specially assigned military duties, or coerces him to violate such a duty, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to seven years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or another particularly serious consequence, |
|---|
|  | b) with a weapon, |
|---|
|  | c) with a group of soldiers, or |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Insult Between Soldiers |
|---|
|

| § 396 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever grossly insults another soldier, if it does not constitute a more serious criminal offense, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 397 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever insults a soldier by violence or threat of imminent violence |
|---|
|  | a) who is a superior or of higher rank, or |
|---|
|  | b) who is a subordinate or of lower rank, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes bodily harm, |
|---|
|  | b) at a time when he himself or the insulted soldier is performing service, |
|---|
|  | c) against a military guard or military police, |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic, or |
|---|
|  | e) with a weapon or with at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 398 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever insults a soldier of the same rank by violence or threat of imminent violence shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes bodily harm, |
|---|
|  | b) at a time when he himself or the insulted soldier is performing service, |
|---|
|  | c) against a military guard or military police, |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic, or |
|---|
|  | e) with a weapon, or with at least two persons. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 399 |

|---|
|  | Violence Against a Superior |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever uses violence, threat of imminent violence or threat of other serious harm against a superior, |
|---|
|  | a) with the intent to influence the performance of his military duties, or |
|---|
|  | b) for the performance of his military duties, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm, |
|---|
|  | b) with a weapon, |
|---|
|  | c) with at least two persons, or |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes death, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 400 |

|---|
|  | Violation of the rights and protected interests of soldiers |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever forces another soldier to perform unauthorised personal services or restricts him in his rights, or arbitrarily makes the performance of his service more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 against a subordinate or a person of lower rank. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) by violence, or threat of violence, or threat of other serious harm, |
|---|
|  | b) with at least two persons, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | Criminal offences against the duty to perform military service |
|---|
|  | Evasion of a service act or performance of military service |
|---|
|

| § 401 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of evading the performance of a service act, injures himself, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 402 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of evading the performance of military service or the fulfilment of military duties, injures himself, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse, or whoever refuses to perform military service or the fulfilment of military duties, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 403 |

|---|
|  | Whoever, during service hours, by consuming alcohol or consuming other addictive substances, brings himself into a state which renders him wholly or partially incapable of performing service duties, even though he has already been punished at least twice for a similar act in the preceding twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|

| § 404 |

|---|
|  | Whoever, during a crisis situation, renders himself, even through negligence, incapable of performing service by consuming alcohol or another addictive substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | Desertion |
|---|
|

| § 405 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever absents himself without authorisation with the intention of evading military service for a long period, or whoever absents himself without authorisation for longer than 30 days, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) with a weapon, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 406 |

|---|
|  | (1) Whoever absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than 48 hours or absents himself repeatedly, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) Whoever absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than 6 days shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he absents himself without authorisation for a period longer than 24 hours during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | (5) Whoever absents himself without authorisation to the enemy shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | Part Three |
|---|
|  | Criminal offences against the duties of guard and supervisory service |
|---|
|

| § 407 |

|---|
|  | Violation of the duties of guard service |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in guard service or other similar service, violates, even through negligence, the regulations or rules of such service, or special orders issued pursuant thereto, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) during the performance of guard service or other similar service which has particularly important state or military significance, |
|---|
|  | b) by a particularly gross violation of his duty, |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation, or |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall be imposed on whoever causes, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, a harmful consequence which the performance of guard service or other similar service was directed at averting. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he intentionally commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during the performance of guard service or other similar service which has particularly important state or military significance during a crisis situation and caused a harmful consequence which the performance of guard service or other similar service was directed at averting. |
|---|
|

| § 408 |

|---|
|  | Violation of the duties of supervisory service |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in supervisory service or other similar service, in a serious manner, even through negligence, violates the regulations or rules of such service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, in supervisory service or other similar service, by inadequate performance of his duties, enables the demanding of personal services, restriction of rights or arbitrary making more difficult of the performance of service of one soldier towards another within the area of competence of such service. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he causes, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, a particularly serious consequence which he was obliged to prevent, or commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 409 |

|---|
|  | Violation of duties in the defence of airspace |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever violates, even through negligence, the regulations or rules of service at radiotechnical posts, in standby units or other facilities designated to ensure the security of airspace, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes a particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|  | Part Four |
|---|
|  | Criminal offences endangering combat capability |
|---|
|

| § 410 |

|---|
|  | Endangering the morale of a unit |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever incites soldiers against military service or against a superior, or whoever otherwise systematically undermines discipline, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 411 |

|---|
|  | Breach of Service Duties |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, to the detriment of a component of the armed forces or an armed corps, by failing to fulfil or by breaching imposed service duties, even through negligence, substantially reduces the usability of armament, equipment, other items or financial resources, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, without authorisation, uses a material resource of small value referred to in paragraph 1 or financial resources of small value for a purpose for which it is not intended, or gives consent to such use, or whoever abuses or enables the abuse of subordinates for non-service tasks, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if |
|---|
|  | a) he procures for himself or another a substantial benefit by an act referred to in paragraph 2, or |
|---|
|  | b) he causes, by an intentionally committed act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, a reduction in combat capability, substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|  | (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits an act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 intentionally during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 412 |

|---|
|  | Cowardice Before the Enemy |
|---|
|  | Whoever, in a combat situation, surrenders into captivity out of cowardice or resignation, shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years. |
|---|
|

| § 413 |

|---|
|  | Failure to Fulfil a Combat Task |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in a combat situation, absents himself without permission, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever, in a combat situation, evades the duty to fulfil a combat task or refuses to use a weapon, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years. |
|---|
|

| § 414 |

|---|
|  | Abandonment of Combat Resources |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, in a combat situation, throws away, abandons or renders unusable a weapon or other war materiel, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits an act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes a particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 415 |

|---|
|  | Surrender of Combat Resources to the Enemy |
|---|
|  | A commander who, without it being necessitated by the combat situation, surrenders to the enemy, even through negligence, military forces or leaves to him fortifications, combat equipment or other combat resources, shall be punished, if he did not act with the intention of supporting the enemy, by imprisonment for three to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | TWELFTH CHAPTER |
|---|
|  | CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST PEACE, AGAINST HUMANITY, CRIMINAL OFFENCES OF TERRORISM, EXTREMISM AND WAR CRIMES |
|---|
|  | Part One |
|---|
|  | Criminal Offences Against Peace and Humanity, Criminal Offences of Terrorism and Extremism |
|---|
|

| § 417 |

|---|
|  | Endangering Peace |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of disrupting peace, in any manner incites to war, propagates war or otherwise supports war propaganda, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment if he commits an act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) in connection with a foreign power or a foreign agent, |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 418 |

|---|
|  | Genocide |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of destroying, in whole or in part, a nation or a national, ethnic, racial or religious group |
|---|
|  | a) causes serious bodily harm or death to a member of such a group, |
|---|
|  | b) implements measures aimed at preventing births within such a group, |
|---|
|  | c) forcibly transfers children from one such group to another, or |
|---|
|  | d) places members of such a group in living conditions intended to bring about its complete or partial physical destruction, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment if he commits an act referred to in paragraph 1 during war or armed conflict. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by a sentence of life imprisonment if he causes the death of several persons by an act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|

| § 419 |

|---|
|  | Terrorist Attack |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, with the intention of damaging the constitutional order or defence capability of the state, disrupting or destroying the basic political, economic or social structure of the state or an international organisation, seriously intimidating the population or compelling the government of a state or another public authority or international organisation to do, omit or tolerate something |
|---|
|  | a) threatens to commit or commits an attack endangering the life, health or personal liberty of a person, |
|---|
|  | b) destroys, disables or damages a public facility, transport system, telecommunications system, information system, including seriously obstructing the functioning of an information system or interrupting the functioning of an information system, a fixed platform on the continental shelf, an energy facility, a water supply facility, a healthcare facility or other important facility, a public space or property, or threatens such conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) disrupts, disables or interrupts the supply of water, electricity or another basic natural resource with the aim of exposing people to danger of death or serious bodily harm or exposing the property of others to danger of large-scale damage, or threatens such conduct, |
|---|
|  | d) seizes an aircraft, ship, other means of passenger transport or freight transport or a fixed platform on the continental shelf, or exercises control over such a means of transport or fixed platform, or destroys or seriously damages a navigation facility or interferes with its operation, or communicates false information, thereby endangering the life or health of people, the safety of such a means of transport, or exposing the property of others to danger of large-scale damage, or threatens such conduct, |
|---|
|  | e) demands, manufactures, obtains, stores, owns, possesses, imports, exports, transports, has transported, supplies or otherwise uses an explosive, nuclear material, radioactive substance, chemical substance, biological agent or toxin, firearm, nuclear weapon, radiological weapon, biological weapon, chemical weapon or other weapon, combat resource or material of a similar nature, or conducts research and development of a nuclear weapon, biological weapon, chemical weapon or other weapon or combat resource or explosive, or a device for the production, processing, storage or use of nuclear materials, radioactive substances, chemical substances or biological agents and toxins, or threatens such conduct, or |
|---|
|  | f) exposes people to danger of death or serious bodily harm, or exposes the property of others to danger of large-scale damage by causing a fire or flood or harmful effects of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or commits other similarly dangerous conduct, or increases such danger or makes it more difficult to avert or mitigate it, or threatens such conduct, shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by a sentence of life imprisonment if he commits an act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or the death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) against a protected person, |
|---|
|  | c) against the armed forces or against the armed corps, |
|---|
|  | d) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or |
|---|
|  | e) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 419a |

|---|
|  | Participation in combat activities of an organized armed group on the territory of another state |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during a war on the territory of another state actively participates in combat activities of an organized armed group shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever |
|---|
|  | a) publicly incites the commission of the criminal offence referred to in paragraph 1, |
|---|
|  | b) requests another to commit or participate in the commission of the act referred to in paragraph 1, |
|---|
|  | c) provides or receives knowledge of methods or techniques for the production or use of explosives, firearms or other weapons, harmful substances or other dangerous substances or other special methods or techniques intended for conducting combat for the purpose of committing the act referred to in paragraph 1, or |
|---|
|  | d) provides financial or other means, services, assistance or creates other conditions for the purpose of committing the criminal offence referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|

| § 419b |

|---|
|  | Certain forms of participation in terrorism |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever publicly incites the commission of any of the criminal offences of terrorism or publicly approves any of the criminal offences of terrorism shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) Whoever |
|---|
|  | a) acquires knowledge of methods or techniques or masters skills for the production, storage, transport or use of explosives, firearms, nuclear weapons, biological weapons, chemical weapons, nuclear materials, radioactive substances, chemical substances, biological agents and toxins or other similarly harmful substances, or dangerous substances for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offences of terrorism, |
|---|
|  | b) provides knowledge of methods or techniques for the production, storage, transport or use of explosives, firearms, nuclear weapons, biological weapons, chemical weapons, nuclear materials, radioactive substances, chemical substances, biological agents and toxins or other similarly harmful substances, or dangerous substances for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offences of terrorism, or |
|---|
|  | c) requests another to commit or participate in the commission of any of the criminal offences of terrorism, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years. |
|---|
|

| § 419c |

|---|
|  | Financing of terrorism |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever, by himself or through another, collects or provides directly or indirectly things, financial means or other means for a perpetrator of terrorism, for a terrorist group, its member, or for the commission of any of the criminal offences of terrorism, or collects things, financial means or other means with the intent that they may be so used, or with the knowledge that they may be used for such purpose, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, by himself or through another, provides things, financial means or other means to a close person of one who participates or participated in the preparation, attempt or commission of any of the criminal offences of terrorism, for participating or having participated in such conduct. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty years shall the perpetrator be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) on a larger scale, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|

| § 419d |

|---|
|  | Travel for the purpose of terrorism |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever travels from the Slovak Republic or through the territory of the Slovak Republic to another state for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offences of terrorism, except the criminal offence of terror under § 313 and 314 and the criminal offence of terrorist attack under § 419, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever travels from another state to the Slovak Republic for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offences of terrorism except the criminal offence of terror under § 313 and 314 and the criminal offence of terrorist attack under § 419. |
|---|
|

| § 420 |

|---|
|  | Torture and other inhuman or cruel treatment |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever in connection with the exercise of authority of a public authority body, at its instigation or with its express or tacit consent, causes physical or mental suffering to another by ill-treatment, torture or other inhuman or cruel treatment shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) By imprisonment for three to ten years shall the perpetrator be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) with at least two persons, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | c) on a protected person, |
|---|
|  | d) from a special motive, or |
|---|
|  | e) on a person whose personal liberty has been restricted in accordance with the law. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years shall the perpetrator be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, |
|---|
|  | b) in order to frustrate or hinder another in the exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms, or |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of a dangerous grouping. |
|---|
|  | (4) By imprisonment for twelve to twenty years shall the perpetrator be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons, or |
|---|
|  | b) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 420a |

|---|
|  | Enforced disappearance |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever as a representative of the state or a person or member of a group acting on the basis of authorization, support or tacit consent of the state detains, imprisons, abducts or otherwise deprives another of liberty and subsequently refuses to confirm the deprivation of liberty or conceals the fate or whereabouts of another and thereby prevents another from exercising legal protection shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever as a superior |
|---|
|  | a) knew or had information indicating that subordinates subject to his authority committed or intended to commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, |
|---|
|  | b) had actual responsibility for activities and control over activities related to the act referred to in paragraph 1, or |
|---|
|  | c) did not take necessary and appropriate measures within his authority to prevent or suppress the act referred to in paragraph 1, or did not report the act referred to in paragraph 1 to the competent authorities for the purpose of investigation or prosecution. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for ten to fifteen years shall be punished whoever |
|---|
|  | a) unlawfully transfers a child on whom the act referred to in paragraph 1 was committed, or a child born during the captivity of a mother on whom the act referred to in paragraph 1 was committed, |
|---|
|  | b) unlawfully transfers a child whose father, mother or legal guardian was subjected to the act referred to in paragraph 1, or |
|---|
|  | c) falsifies, conceals or destroys documents confirming the true identity of the child referred to in letter a) or b). |
|---|
|  | (4) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years shall the perpetrator be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraphs 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) on a protected person, or |
|---|
|  | b) thereby causes serious bodily harm or death. |
|---|
|  | Support and promotion of groups aimed at suppressing fundamental rights and freedoms |
|---|
|

| § 421 |

|---|
|  | Establishment, Support and Promotion of a Movement Aimed at Suppressing Fundamental Rights and Freedoms |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever establishes, supports or promotes a group, movement or ideology that aims to suppress the fundamental rights and freedoms of persons, or which proclaims racial, ethnic, national or religious hatred, or whoever promotes a group, movement or ideology that in the past aimed to suppress the fundamental rights and freedoms of persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) publicly or in a place accessible to the public, |
|---|
|  | b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | c) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 422 |

|---|
|  | Expression of Sympathy for a Movement Aimed at Suppressing Fundamental Rights and Freedoms |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever publicly or in a place accessible to the public, in particular by using flags, badges, uniforms or slogans, expresses sympathy for a group, movement or ideology that aims or in the past aimed to suppress the fundamental rights and freedoms of persons, or which proclaims racial, ethnic, national, religious hatred, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, in the act referred to in paragraph 1, uses modified flags, badges, uniforms or slogans that create the appearance of genuine ones. |
|---|
|

| § 422a |

|---|
|  | Production of Extremist Material |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever produces extremist material or participates in such production shall be punished by imprisonment for three to six years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or |
|---|
|  | b) as a member of an extremist group. |
|---|
|

| § 422b |

|---|
|  | Distribution of Extremist Material |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever reproduces, transports, procures, makes accessible, puts into circulation, imports, exports, offers, sells, sends or distributes extremist material shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) by a more serious manner of conduct, |
|---|
|  | b) publicly, or
|
|---|
|  | c) as a member of an extremist group.
|
|---|
|

| § 422c |

|---|
|  | Possession of Extremist Material |
|---|
|  | Whoever possesses extremist material shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|

| §422d |

|---|
|  | Denial and Approval of the Holocaust, Crimes of Political Regimes and Crimes Against Humanity |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever publicly denies, questions, approves or attempts to justify the Holocaust, crimes of a regime based on fascist ideology, crimes of a regime based on communist ideology or crimes of another similar movement that by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm aims to suppress the fundamental rights and freedoms of persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever publicly denies, approves, questions, grossly trivialises or attempts to justify genocide, crimes against peace, crimes against humanity or war crimes in a manner that may incite violence or hatred against a group of persons or a member thereof, if the perpetrator or participant in such act has been convicted by a final judgment of an international court established under public international law whose jurisdiction has been recognised by the Slovak Republic, or by a final judgment of a court of the Slovak Republic. |
|---|
|

| § 423 |

|---|
|  | Defamation of a Nation, Race and Belief |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever publicly defames |
|---|
|  | a) a nation, its language, a race or an ethnic group, or |
|---|
|  | b) a group of persons or an individual for their actual or presumed affiliation with a race, nation, nationality, ethnic group, for their actual or presumed origin, colour, religious belief or because they have no belief, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of an extremist group, |
|---|
|  | b) as a public official, or |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive. |
|---|
|

| § 424 |

|---|
|  | Incitement to National, Racial and Ethnic Hatred |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever publicly incites violence or hatred against a group of persons or an individual for their actual or presumed affiliation with a race, nation, nationality, ethnic group, for their actual or presumed origin, colour, sexual orientation, religious belief or because they have no belief, or publicly incites the restriction of their rights and freedoms, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever conspires or assembles to commit the act referred to in paragraph 1. |
|---|
|  | (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 |
|---|
|  | a) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | b) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | c) as a member of an extremist group, or |
|---|
|  | d) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 424a |

|---|
|  | Apartheid and Discrimination of a Group of Persons |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever practises apartheid or racial, ethnic, national or religious segregation, or other widespread or systematic discrimination of a group of persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) as a member of an extremist group, |
|---|
|  | b) as a public official, |
|---|
|  | c) from a special motive, |
|---|
|  | d) and thereby subjects such group of persons to inhuman or degrading treatment, |
|---|
|  | e) and thereby exposes such group of persons to danger of serious injury to health or death, or |
|---|
|  | f) during a crisis situation. |
|---|
|

| § 425 |

|---|
|  | Inhumanity |
|---|
|  | Whoever, as part of a widespread or systematic attack directed against a civilian population, commits |
|---|
|  | a) murder, |
|---|
|  | b) extermination of people, |
|---|
|  | c) enslavement, |
|---|
|  | d) deportation or forcible transfer of population, |
|---|
|  | e) torture, |
|---|
|  | f) rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, unlawful sterilisation or other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity, |
|---|
|  | g) persecution of a group of population on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural or religious grounds, on grounds of gender or other similar grounds, |
|---|
|  | h) imprisonment or other serious deprivation of personal liberty in violation of fundamental rules of international law or any other restriction of personal liberty resulting in enforced disappearance of persons, |
|---|
|  | i) apartheid or other similar segregation or discrimination, or |
|---|
|  | j) other inhuman act, shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment. |
|---|
|  | Part Two |
|---|
|  | War Crimes |
|---|
|

| § 426 |

|---|
|  | Use of prohibited means of combat and unlawful conduct of hostilities |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during war orders |
|---|
|  | a) the use of a prohibited means of combat or material of a similar nature, or uses such means or material, or |
|---|
|  | b) the conduct of hostilities by a prohibited method, or himself conducts hostilities in such manner, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same way as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever as a commander, in violation of the provisions of international law on means and methods of warfare, intentionally |
|---|
|  | a) causes harm by military operation to civilian persons in life, health or property, or conducts an attack against them by way of reprisals, conducts an attack against an undefended place or demilitarized zone, |
|---|
|  | b) destroys or damages a dam, nuclear power plant or similar installation containing dangerous forces, or |
|---|
|  | c) destroys or damages an object dedicated to humanitarian purposes or an internationally recognized cultural or natural monument. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes |
|---|
|  | a) serious injury to health of several persons or death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 427 |

|---|
|  | Pillage in the area of military operations |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever in the area of military operations, on the battlefield, in places affected by military operations or in occupied territory |
|---|
|  | a) appropriates another's property, taking advantage of someone's distress, |
|---|
|  | b) arbitrarily destroys another's property or takes it under the pretext of military necessity, or |
|---|
|  | c) robs the fallen or wounded, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) using violence, threat of violence or other serious harm, or |
|---|
|  | b) against persons or property enjoying special protection under law or the rules of international law. |
|---|
|  | (3) In the same way as in paragraph 2 the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes |
|---|
|  | a) serious injury to health, or |
|---|
|  | b) substantial damage. |
|---|
|  | (4) By imprisonment for ten to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes |
|---|
|  | a) death, or |
|---|
|  | b) damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 428 |

|---|
|  | Misuse of internationally recognized markings and state symbols |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during war misuses the marking of the Red Cross or other distinctive signs or colours recognized by international law for the designation of medical institutions, means of transport, persons providing medical assistance or evacuation, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same way as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever during war misuses the emblem of the United Nations, the national flag, state emblem, military insignia, insignia or uniform of a neutral state or another state which is not a party to the war. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for twelve to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes as a means of perfidy |
|---|
|  | a) death of several persons, |
|---|
|  | b) damage on a large scale, or |
|---|
|  | c) another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 429 |

|---|
|  | Attack against a parlementaire |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever insults a parlementaire or a member of his escort or whoever unlawfully detains such person shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. |
|---|
|  | (2) By imprisonment for four to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious injury to health to the parlementaire or a member of his escort. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes death to the parlementaire or a member of his escort. |
|---|
|

| § 430 |

|---|
|  | Abuse of the right of requisition |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during war |
|---|
|  | a) abuses the authority entrusted to him in the field of military requisition, |
|---|
|  | b) carries out requisition although he was not authorized to do so, or |
|---|
|  | c) refuses to issue a certificate of the type and quantity of property delivered in military requisition, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. |
|---|
|  | (2) By imprisonment for three to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes substantial damage, |
|---|
|  | b) using violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm, or |
|---|
|  | c) against persons or property enjoying special protection under law or the rules of international law. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for five to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale. |
|---|
|

| § 431 |

|---|
|  | War cruelty |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during war violates the rules of international law by treating cruelly the defenceless civilian population, refugees, wounded, members of armed forces who have already laid down their arms, or prisoners of war, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same way as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever during war violates the rules of international law by |
|---|
|  | a) failing to take effective measures to protect persons who need such assistance, in particular children, women, wounded or elderly persons, or obstructs such measures, or |
|---|
|  | b) prevents or obstructs civil defence organizations of the enemy, neutral or other state in the performance of their humanitarian tasks. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes serious injury to health or death or another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 432 |

|---|
|  | Persecution of the population |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever during war commits inhumane acts arising from national, racial or ethnic discrimination or terrorizes the defenceless civilian population by violence or threat of its use shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years. |
|---|
|  | (2) In the same way as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever in the period referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) destroys or seriously impairs a source of basic necessities of life of the civilian population in occupied territory or in a contact zone, or arbitrarily fails to provide the population with assistance necessary for survival, |
|---|
|  | b) unjustifiably delays the return of the civilian population or prisoners of war, |
|---|
|  | c) unjustifiably displaces the civilian population of occupied territory, |
|---|
|  | d) settles occupied territory with the population of his own country, or |
|---|
|  | e) arbitrarily prevents the civilian population or prisoners of war from having their criminal acts decided in fair proceedings. |
|---|
|  | (3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes serious injury to health or death or another particularly serious consequence. |
|---|
|

| § 433 |

|---|
|  | War Crimes |
|---|
|  | (1) Whoever commits an act considered by Article 8 of the Rome Statute of the International Criminal Court to be a war crime shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment. |
|---|
|  | (2) The offender shall be punished by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 |
|---|
|  | a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or the death of several persons or another particularly serious consequence, or |
|---|
|  | b) for remuneration. |
|---|
|

| § 434 |

|---|
|  | Endangering Cultural Property |
|---|
|  | Whoever during war |
|---|
|  | a) destroys or appropriates on a large scale objects of cultural value protected by international convention, |
|---|
|  | b) commits plunder, vandalism or other abuse against such an object, |
|---|
|  | c) makes such an object the target of an attack in violation of international convention, or |
|---|
|  | d) uses an object of cultural value under enhanced protection of international convention or its immediate surroundings in violation of international convention to support military activity, |
|---|
|  | shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. |
|---|
|  | Part Three |
|---|
|  | Common Provisions |
|---|
|

| § 435 |

|---|
|  | (1) For the purposes of applying the provisions of this chapter, war means |
|---|
|  | a) an international armed conflict, or |
|---|
|  | b) a protracted armed conflict on the territory of a state between governmental authorities and organized armed groups or between such groups inter se, excluding internal disturbances and tensions such as riots, isolated and sporadic acts of violence or other acts of a similar nature. |
|---|
|  | (2) A military commander also means a person acting de facto as a military commander. |
|---|
|  | (3) A military commander is criminally responsible for the criminal offences referred to in this chapter even if they were committed by armed forces under his de facto command and control as a result of his failure to control such forces, if |
|---|
|  | a) he knew or, owing to the circumstances at the time, should have known that the armed forces were committing or were about to commit such criminal offences, and |
|---|
|  | b) he failed to take all necessary and reasonable measures within his power to prevent or repress their commission or failed to submit the matter to the competent authorities for investigation and prosecution. |
|---|
|  | (4) A superior, with the exception of a superior referred to in paragraph 3, is criminally responsible for the criminal offences referred to in this chapter even if they were committed by his subordinates under his de facto authority and control as a result of his failure to properly control such subordinates, if |
|---|
|  | a) he knew or consciously disregarded information which clearly indicated that the subordinates were committing or were about to commit such criminal offences, |
|---|
|  | b) the criminal offences concerned activities that were within the de facto responsibility and control of the superior, and |
|---|
|  | c) he failed to take all necessary and reasonable measures within his power to prevent or repress their commission or failed to submit the matter to the competent authorities for investigation and prosecution. |
|---|
|  |
|
|---|
|  | PART THREE |
|---|
|  | COMMON, TRANSITIONAL AND FINAL PROVISIONS |
|---|
|

| § 436 |

|---|
|  | Common Provision |
|---|
|  | This Act transposes the legally binding acts of the European Union listed in the Annex. |
|---|
|  | Transitional Provisions |
|---|
|

| § 437 |

|---|
|  | (1) If it is necessary to determine whether a criminal offence under Act No. 140/1961 Coll. the Criminal Code as amended exhibits the characteristics of a misdemeanour, felony or particularly serious felony under this Act, the provisions of § 10 and § 11 of this Act shall apply depending on the form of culpability and the sentence specified in Act No. 140/1961 Coll. the Criminal Code as amended. |
|---|
|  | (2) In assessing the fulfilment of conditions for deciding on conditional release from the execution of a sentence of imprisonment or deciding on conditional waiver of the execution of the remainder of the sentence for sentences imposed after the entry into force of this Act for a criminal offence under Act No. 140/1961 Coll. the Criminal Code as amended, this Act shall apply. |
|---|
|  | (3) In assessing the fulfilment of conditions for deciding on conditional release from the execution of a sentence of imprisonment or deciding on conditional waiver of the execution of the remainder of the sentence for sentences imposed before the entry into force of this Act, the previous regulations shall apply. A person sentenced to an exceptional sentence of imprisonment under § 43 paragraph 1 of Act No. 140/1961 Coll. the Criminal Code as amended may be conditionally released only after serving three quarters of the imposed sentence of imprisonment; a person sentenced under this provision to an exceptional sentence of life imprisonment may not be conditionally released. |
|---|
|  | (4) If the limitation period for criminal prosecution or the limitation period for the execution of a sentence expired before the entry into force of this Act under the previous regulations, the provisions on the limitation period for criminal prosecution and the limitation period for the execution of a sentence under this Act shall not apply. |
|---|
|  | (5) The provision of § 47 paragraph 2 also applies to a person who was punished for any of the criminal offences referred to in § 43 paragraph 1 of Act No. 140/1961 Coll. the Criminal Code as amended, if the person committed another criminal offence referred to in § 47 paragraph 2 after the entry into force of this Act. |
|---|
|  | (6) The court may decide pursuant to § 34 paragraph 8 letter d) even if the offender was punished for any of the criminal offences referred to in § 43 paragraph 1 of Act No. 140/1961 Coll. the Criminal Code as amended, which was committed before the entry into force of this Act. |
|---|
|

| § 438 |

|---|
|  | (1) Where this Act refers to an institution for the execution of a sentence of minimum, medium or maximum security level, this means the first, second or third correctional group established under the previous regulations. |
|---|
|  | (2) Where generally binding legal regulations promulgated before the entry into force of this Act use the term particularly serious criminal offence, this means a felony under this Act. |
|---|
|

| § 438a |

|---|
|  | Transitional Provisions for Amendments Effective from 20 December 2008 |
|---|
|  | The provision of § 269a shall apply only if the offender or seller was sanctioned by a previous decision after 19 December 2008. |
|---|
|

| § 438b |

|---|
|  | Transitional Provisions for Amendments Effective from 1 January 2009 |
|---|
|  | If the punishability of an act is assessed and a sentence is imposed under the law effective before 1 January 2009, a monetary penalty shall be imposed from 1 January 2009 in such a way that its amount expressed in Slovak crowns is converted to euros according to the conversion rate and rounded according to the rounding rules and other rules for the transition to the euro established by a generally binding legal regulation. |
|---|
|

| § 438c |

|---|
|  | Transitional Provision for Amendments Effective from 1 September 2010 |
|---|
|  | Forfeiture of a monetary amount under § 83a and forfeiture of property under § 83b may be imposed on a legal person only if the criminal offence referred to in § 83a paragraph 1 or § 83b paragraph 1 was committed, even at the stage of attempt, or if participation in a criminal offence occurred after 31 August 2010. |
|---|
|

| § 438d |

|---|
|  | Transitional Provision for Amendments Effective from 1 November 2011 |
|---|
|  | The provision of § 61 paragraphs 3 and 5 also applies to a person who was convicted of any of the criminal offences referred to in § 61 paragraph 3 or 5 before 1 November 2011, if that person committed another criminal offence referred to in § 61 paragraph 3 or 5 after 1 November 2011. However, the court need not take into account a conviction imposed before 1 November 2011 if, in view of the exceptional circumstances of the previous cases, the application of the sentence range for the prohibition of activities established in § 61 paragraph 3 or 5 would be disproportionately severe for the offender. |
|---|
|

| § 438e |

|---|
|  | Transitional provision for amendments effective from 1 January 2013 |
|---|
|  | A conviction by a court of another Member State of the European Union pursuant to § 7b(2) shall not be taken into account if it became final before 1 January 2013. |
|---|
|

| § 438f |

|---|
|  | Transitional provision for amendments effective from 1 August 2013 |
|---|
|  | (1) A penalty of expulsion imposed under Act No. 140/1961 Coll. Criminal Code as amended shall be deemed to have been executed after fifteen years from the order for its execution; the provision of § 93(2) is not affected by this. |
|---|
|  | (2) The execution of the penalty of expulsion pursuant to subsection 1 shall be notified to the criminal records by the court which decided the case at first instance, at the request of the convicted person or even without such request. |
|---|
|

| § 438g |

|---|
|  | Transitional provision for amendments effective from 1 January 2016 |
|---|
|  | The provisions of this Act on monitoring by technical means effective from 1 January 2016 shall not apply to penalties, appropriate restrictions and obligations and educational restrictions and obligations imposed with final effect before 1 January 2016. |
|---|
|

| § 438h |

|---|
|  | Transitional provision for amendments effective from 1 August 2019 |
|---|
|  | The provisions of § 67(2) and § 68(1) as worded with effect from 1 August 2019 shall also apply to a convicted person who was imposed a sentence of life imprisonment pursuant to § 34(8) or § 67(3) as worded with effect until 31 July 2019. |
|---|
|

| § 438i |

|---|
|  | Transitional provision for amendments effective from 1 January 2021 |
|---|
|  | (1) In assessing the fulfilment of conditions for issuing a decision on the imposition of a protective measure of confiscation of part of property pursuant to § 83a, when determining the amount of gross disproportion between the value of the property of the offender which he acquired or transferred or had transferred to another person and his income acquired in accordance with the law, only the value of property which the offender acquired or transferred to another person from 1 January 2021 shall be taken into account. |
|---|
|  | (2) Where the term "legalisation of proceeds from criminal activity" is used in all forms in generally binding legal regulations, it shall be understood to mean "legalisation of proceeds from criminal activity" in the appropriate form. |
|---|
|

| § 438j |

|---|
|  | Transitional provision for amendments effective from 1 May 2022 |
|---|
