|  | A sentence of imprisonment which was not conditionally suspended and which was imposed with final effect before 1 May 2022 for an act which was assessed as a criminal offence pursuant to § 171 or § 172, and which would have been assessed after 30 April 2022 as another criminal offence or as a less severely punishable act, shall be proportionally reduced by the court after reassessment if the length of the imposed sentence of imprisonment exceeds the upper limit of the sentencing range for such an act after the entry into force of this Act, or if, having regard to the person of the offender and the circumstances of the case, maintaining the original length of the sentence of imprisonment would be disproportionately severe for the convicted person. In doing so, the court shall take into account the mutual ratio of the sentencing range established for the act before and after the entry into force of this Act. The same principles shall be followed if an aggregate or cumulative sentence was imposed for such an act and a concurrent other criminal offence. The proportional reduction of the sentence shall be decided by the court in whose district the sentence of imprisonment is being executed, even without a motion. |
|---|
|  | Final provisions |
|---|
|

| § 439 |

|---|
|  | The following are repealed: |
|---|
|  | 1. Act No. 165/1950 Coll. on the protection of peace, |
|---|
|  | 2. Act No. 140/1961 Coll. Criminal Code as amended by Act No. 53/1963 Coll., Act No. 184/1964 Coll., Act No. 56/1965 Coll., Act No. 81/1966 Coll., Act No. 148/1969 Coll., Act No. 45/1973 Coll., Act No. 43/1980 Coll., Legislative Measure No. 10/1989 Coll., Act No. 159/1989 Coll., Act No. 47/1990 Coll., Act No. 84/1990 Coll., Act No. 175/1990 Coll., Act No. 457/1990 Coll., Act No. 545/1990 Coll., Act No. 490/1991 Coll., Act No. 557/1991 Coll., the finding of the Constitutional Court of the Czech and Slovak Federative Republic of 4 September 1992 published in part 93/1992 Coll., Act of the National Council of the Slovak Republic No. 177/1993 Z. z., Act of the National Council of the Slovak Republic No. 248/1994 Z. z., Act of the National Council of the Slovak Republic No. 102/1995 Z. z., Act of the National Council of the Slovak Republic No. 233/1995 Z. z., Act of the National Council of the Slovak Republic No. 100/1996 Z. z., Act No. 13/1998 Z. z., Act No. 129/1998 Z. z., Act No. 10/1999 Z. z., Act No. 183/1999 Z. z., Act No. 399/2000 Z. z., Act No. 253/2001 Z. z., Act No. 485/2001 Z. z., Act No. 237/2002 Z. z., Act No. 421/2002 Z. z., Act No. 448/2002 Z. z., Act No. 553/2002 Z. z., Act No. 457/2003 Z. z., Act No. 403/2004 Z. z., Act No. 576/2004 Z. z., Act No. 613/2004 Z. z., Act No. 757/2004 Z. z. and Act No. 227/2005 Z. z., |
|---|
|  | 3. Act No. 120/1962 Coll. on the fight against alcoholism, |
|---|
|  | 4. Act No. 448/2002 Z. z. on protective supervision and amending Act No. 140/1961 Coll. Criminal Code as amended, as amended by Act No. 550/2003 Z. z. |
|---|
|

| § 440 |

|---|
|  | This Act shall enter into force on 1 January 2006. |

|  | 2. Council Framework Decision 2000/383/JHA of 29 May 2000 on increasing protection by criminal penalties and other sanctions against counterfeiting in connection with the introduction of the euro (Special edition of the OJ EU, Chapter 19/Vol. 1; OJ EC L 140, 14. 6. 2000) as amended by Council Framework Decision 2001/888/JHA of 6 December 2001 (Special edition of the OJ EU, Chapter 19/Vol. 4; OJ EC L 329, 14. 12. 2001). |

|---|

|---|
|  | 8. Council Framework Decision 2003/568/JHA of 22 July 2003 on combating corruption in the private sector (Special edition of the OJ EU, Chapter 19/Vol. 6; OJ EU L 192, 31. 7. 2003). |
|---|
|  | 9. Council Framework Decision 2004/757/JHA of 25 October 2004 laying down minimum provisions on the constituent elements of criminal acts and penalties in the field of illicit drug trafficking (OJ EU L 335, 11. 11. 2004). |
|---|
|  | 10. Council Framework Decision 2008/913/JHA of 28 November 2008 on combating certain forms and expressions of racism and xenophobia by means of criminal law (OJ EU L 328, 6. 12. 2008). |
|---|
|  | 11. Council Framework Decision 2005/222/JHA of 24 February 2005 on attacks against information systems (OJ EU L 69, 16. 3. 2005). |
|---|
|  | 12. Council Framework Decision 2008/919/JHA of 28 November 2008 amending Framework Decision 2002/475/JHA on combating terrorism (OJ EU L 330, 9. 12. 2008). |
|---|
|  | 13. Council Framework Decision 2008/841/JHA of 24 October 2008 on the fight against organised crime (OJ EU L 300, 11. 11. 2008). |
|---|
|  | 14. Directive 2008/99/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 19 November 2008 on the protection of the environment through criminal law (OJ EU L 328, 6. 12. 2008). |
|---|
